80 viḻukkāṭu taṭavikku mēl varum kuṟiyīṭukaḷ 67 maṭṭumē yākum. (pātikku mēṟpaṭṭa kuṟiyīṭukaḷ ovvoṉṟum pattu taṭavikkum kuṟivākavē varukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷḷum 112 ovvoṉṟum oru taṭaviyē varukiṟatu. āka, inta 7 kuṟiyīṭu kaḷukkum mutaṉmi tantu vācikka muṟpaṭuvatu nallatu eṉkiṟār makātēvaṉ. mottam cumār 400 kuṟiyīṭukaḷil aṭippaṭik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ 200 eṉpatu parpōlā karuttu.
36. cintuveḷi muttiri eḻuttukkaḷ paḻantamiḻē (paḻan tirāviṭamē eṉpati ittuṟi vallunar aṉivarum ēṟ kiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟip paṭittuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷ (parpōlā, makā tēvaṉ, mativāṇaṉ, pūrṇa cantira jīvā, pēr carvīs mutali yōr) ovvoruvarum ovvoru vakiyāka vācit tuḷḷaṉar. muttiriyik kuttiya piṉ kiṭikkum muttirip pativil uḷḷa vācakaṅkaḷi (Impressions) valamiruntu iṭamākavē perumpālōr paṭikkiṉṟaṉar. (mativāṇaṉ maṭṭum iṭamiruntu valamākap paṭittuḷḷār. eṉiṉum im muttiri eḻuttukkaḷ, tirāviṭa moḻiyic cārntatākat tāṉ irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu paṟṟi maṭṭum aṟiñarkaḷiṭam orumitta karuttu uḷḷatu; piṉvaruvaṉavaṟṟik kāṇka:
sṭāṉli vārparṭ : intiyāvukku ōr aṟimukam eṉṟa nūlil uḷḷa "pala vakiyāṉa ātāraṅkaḷiliruntum nām karutuvatu avarkaḷ tol tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉpatum, tamiḻukkup pāṭṭaṉ muṟiyākak kūṭiya oru moḻi avarkaḷ moḻiyāka iruntirukkalām eṉpatum ākum."
“We assume from various shreds of evidence that they are were proto Dravidian, possibly using a language that was a grandfather of modern Tamil” (Stanley Wolpert: An Introduction to India, University of California Press 1991)
jē.em. rāparṭs : peṅkuyiṉ ulaka varalāṟu "teṉ ṉintiyāvil iṉṟum vaḻaṅkivarum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷōṭu iyipuṭiya oru moḻiyic cārntaviyāka (cintuveḷi muttiric coṟkaḷ) irukkalāmeṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu.''
It now seems at least likely that they are part of a language akin to the Dravidian tongues still used in southern India (J.M. Roberts History of the World Pelican 1992).
kamil cuvelapil : tirāviṭa moḻiyiyal - ōr aṟimukam ( "cintu veḷi eḻuttukkaḷiṉ moḻi eṉṉa eṉṟu iṟutiyāka niṟuvappaṭum poḻutu atu tirāviṭa moḻi cārntatāka amivataṟkē vāyppu mika atikam.''
The most probable candidate is and remins some form of Dravidian” (Dravidian Linguistics - An Introduction. Pondicherry 1990; Chap VI : Dravidian and Harappan)
37. cintu veḷi muttiri eḻuttukkaḷiliruntē caṅka kālat tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ uruvāyiṉa eṉpatu mativāṇaṉ karuttu. ēṟattāḻa ki.mu. 700kku muṉṉamē tamiḻukku ōr eḻuttu vari vaṭivam iruntatu; atip piṉpaṟṟiyē acōka pirāmi eḻuttu uruvākkappaṭṭatu; eṉak koḷvataṟ kāṉa ātāraṅ kaḷ uṇṭu. 2006 pipravari mātattil mayilāṭutuṟi - cem piyaṉ kaṇṭiyūril vi. caṇmukanātaṉ kaṇṭeṭutta putuk kaṟkālak karuvi oṉṟil cintuveḷi eḻuttukkaḷ nāṉku uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi ai. makātēvaṉ "muruku aṉ" (murukaṉ) eṉṟu paṭittuḷḷār. akkaruviyiṉ kālam ki.mu. 2000 -1500 āka irukkalām eṉṟu avar karutukiṟār. putukkaṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷum akkālattil vāḻnta cintuveḷi nākarika makkaḷum orē moḻiyiyē pēciṉār eṉṟum ammoḻi tirāviṭa moḻiyē eṉṟum ik kaṇṭupiṭippu niṟuvukiṟatu eṉpatum avar karuttu . ('The Neolithic people of Tamilnadu and the Indus Valley people shared the same language which can only be Dravidian and not Indo-Aryan"); 15 2006. intu nāḷitaḻ.
e. varalāṟṟuk kālattukku caṅka kālattukku muntiya (Proto Historic) kāla tamiḻaka varalāṟu (ki.mu. 1000 - ki.mu. 300) 
38. cīṉivācar tam nūliṉ mutal āṟu iyalkaḷil ki.mu. 4000 - ki.mu. 1000 kāla aḷavil tamiḻaka nākarikam evvāṟu vaḷarn tirukkum eṉṟa taṉatu uṉṉippi virivākat tantuḷḷār. avvāṟē vaḷarntirukkalām. avar karuttu iṉṟiya nili yil potuvāka varalāṟṟaṟiñar - māntaviyalāḷar ēṟṟuḷḷa karuttu alla. eṉiṉum kuṟiñci - mulli - neytal - marutam eṉṟa muṟippaṭitāṉ tamiḻakattil (ēṉ ulakam muḻuvataṟkum itu poruntum eṉpār cīṉivācar) uruvākiyirukkum eṉṟa kōṭpāṭu orēyaṭiyāka otukkiviṭak kūṭiyataṉṟu; mēlum cintikkattakkatēyeṉka. iyalkaḷ 4lilum luēm irāmāyaṇa pārata itikācak katikaḷi varalāṟṟu nikaḻvukaḷāka cīṉi vācar koṇṭuḷḷatiyum iṉṟu yārum ēṟpatilli. itu kuṟittu cuvirā jeysvāl (1974) kūṟuvatu varumāṟu.
"mikap perumpāṉmi tirāviṭarkaḷik koṇṭa teṉṉāṭṭuc camūkattil tāṅkaḷ 'aya lakattār' eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭati ēṟṟuk koḷḷa teṉṉintiya pirāmaṇa varalāṟṟaṟiñar iṇaṅka villi. (avarkaḷ virumpiyavāṟu) teṉṉintiya varalāṟṟi uruvākkiṭa avarkaḷ rāmāyaṇa - pārata itikācap puṉikatikaḷiyum purāṇak kaṟpaṉikaḷiyum, kuruṭṭuttaṉamākavum āyvuk kaṇṇōṭṭam iṉṟiyum ātāramākak koṇṭa ṉar. intak kuṟipāṭukaḷ pi.ṭi. cīṉivāca aiyaṅ kāruṭiya "tamiḻar varalāṟṟil atikam."
"South Indian Brahmans were not prepared to accept at its face value the contention that they constituted "a foreign element” in an overwhelmingly Dravidian population"
XXXXX
a great deal of blind and uncritical reliance was placed on epic myths and Puranic stories for the reconstruction of the history of the south. P.T. Srinivasa Iyengars's History of the Tamils suffers heavily from Such defects".
39. tamiḻaka varalāṟṟil varalāṟṟuk kālat toṭakkam eṉa potuvāka iṉṟu ēṟkappaṭum caṅka kālam kimu. 300 - kipi. 300 cārntatu. ataṟku muntiya varalāṟṟukku muntiya kālam" ākiya ki.mu. 1000 - 300 paṟṟiya ceytikaḷit tiṟampaṭat taruvatu rājaṉ (2004) tolliyal nōkkil caṅkakālam ākum. āticcanallūril uḷḷa ki.mu. 1000 kālattiya iṭukāṭu 114 ēkkar parappuḷḷatu. atil 1 viḻukkāṭu maṭṭumē akaḻvāyvu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. koṭumaṇalil (caṅkakālak koṭumaṇam ) 50 ēkkar parappuḷḷa paḻiya vāḻviṭattilum 1 viḻukkāṭu maṭṭumē akaḻvāyvu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. inta oru viḻuk kāṭu akaḻvāyvukaḷil kiṭitta eccaṅkaḷiliruntē kimu 1000 mutal tamiḻakattil vāḻnta makkaḷē, iṭiyīṭiṉṟi, caṅka ilakkiyam vivarikkum tamiḻar camutāya makkaḷāyiṉar eṉṟu niṟuva iyaṉṟuḷḷa tu. makātēvaṉ (2003) Early Tamil Epigraphy nūlil tamiḻik kalveṭṭukaḷ evaṟṟiyum ki.mu. 200kku muntiyatākak karutavilli. āṉāl avaṟṟi (kuṟippāka koṭumaṇal pāṉi ōṭṭut tamiḻp poṟippukaḷi) kimu 4 allatu 5 nūṟṟāṇṭiṉavākat tāṉ koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉpataṟkāṉa ātāraṅkaḷi rājaṉ tantuḷḷār. mēlē pakuti āṟiyum kīḻē patti 42 yiyum kāṇka.
40. kimu 1000 - kimu. 300 kālakaṭṭamē tamiḻakattiṉ irumpukkālam allatu peruṅkaṟpaṭic ciṉṉak (Megalithic) kālam eṉṟu kuṟippiṭap paṭukiṟatu. akkāla kaṭṭattil iṟanta varkaḷ tāḻiyil putikkappaṭṭaṉar. kaṟpatukkikaḷil aṭak kam ceyyappaṭṭaṉar. peruṅkaṟpaṭic ciṉṉaṅkaḷ cila viṭaṅkaḷil iruntaṉa; cila viṭaṅkaḷil illi ; eṉiṉum ikkālam "peruṅkaṟpaṭic ciṉṉakkālam" eṉṟē potu vāka kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu.
41. ki.mu. 1000 aḷavil makkaḷ perumaḷavukku āṭumāṭu vaḷarppavarkaḷākavē iruntaṉar. vēḷāṇmi toṭakka niliyil iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu akaḻvāyvukaḷiṉ muṭivākum. koṭumaṇal, tāṇṭikkuṭi pāṉi ōṭṭut tamiḻiṉ poṟippuk kālam ki.mu. 5 allatu 4 ām nūṟṟāṇṭākattāṉ irukka vēṇṭum eṉa rājaṉ niṟuviyuḷḷār. appoḻutē eḻuttaṟivu peṟṟa camutāyam tamiḻakattil iruntatu. eṉavē varalāṟṟuk kālamē tamiḻakattil ki.mu. 500liruntu toṭaṅki yatākak koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu eṉpār rājaṉ.
42. tamiḻiya moḻi pēcunar kimu 2000kku muṉṉar iruntē tamiḻakattilum teṉṉāṭṭilum iṭiviṭātu vacittuvanta maṇṇiṉ mintarē eṉpatiyē tolliyal āyvukaḷ - kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpati ālciṉ tampatiyar 1988 tam nūlil piṉvarumāṟu kūṟuvar.
"teṉṉintiyā iṉṟu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkum pakuti. ammoḻikaḷip pēcuvōr pūrvīkam eṉṉavāka iruntirukkalām? avarkaḷ (vaṭamēṟkiliruntu) teṉṉintiyā viṟku vantu kuṭiyēṟumpōtu tammuṭaṉ irumpiyum peruṅ kaṟpaṭi nākarikattiyum koṇarntaṉar eṉa (valuvāṉa ātāramiṉṟik) kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. tolliyal cāṉṟukaḷiṉpaṭi ippaṭi naṭantirukka vāyppu mikak kuṟivu. irumpup payaṉ pāṭṭiṟku 1000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar iruntē karunāṭakat til (teṉ intiyāvil piṟa pakutikaḷilum kūṭa irukkalām). peruṅkaṟpaṭi nākarikak kuṭiyiruppukaḷ iruntuḷḷaṉa; avi iṭiyīṭu iṉṟi toṭarntu iruntu vantuḷḷaṉa ; viyakkat takka paṇpāṭṭut toṭarcciyuṭaṉ iṉṟum uḷḷa paṇ pāṭṭuk kūṟukaḷ pala antap paḻiya nākarikak kūṟukaḷiṉ toṭarcciyākavē tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. uṭaṟkūṟu amippilum antap paḻiya nākarika makkaḷum iṉṟiya makkaḷum perum aḷavukku oṉṟu pōlavē uḷḷaṉar. eṉavē teṉ ṉintiyāvil (pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar iruntē) putukkaṟkāla nākarikam paṭitta makkaḷiṭiyē uruvāṉaviyē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ eṉpati maṟuppatu kaṭiṉamāka uḷḷatu. teṉṉintiyāvil paṟpala pakutikaḷil irunta putukkaṟkāla nākarika makkaḷiṭiyē toṭarpu iruntirukkum, potuvāṉa tirāviṭa moḻiyum iruntirukkum. ki.mu. 3000 - 1000 kāla aḷavil teṉṉintiyāvil irunta putuk kaṟkāla kuṭiyiruppu kaḷil iruntu piṟantaviyē varalāṟṟuk kālat toṭak kattic cārnta kuṭiyiruppukaḷum eṉalām."
The southern part of the peninsula is today the homeland of the Dravidian language, and we may well enquire what - speaking in broadest terms - is likely to have been their history. It has been climed, though not on very solid grounds, that the earliest speakers of these languages brought with them into Peninsular India both iron and the custom of making megalithic graves. In the light of archeological evidence this appears to be extremely improbable. We now know that at least for a millennium prior to the arrival of iron, there were established settlements in Karnataka, and probably also in other parts of the peninsula, and these settlements show evidence of a remarkable continuity of culture. Many modern culture trits appear to derive from them, and a substantial part of the populations shows physical affiliation to the Neolithic people. In the light of all this it is difficult to believe that the Dravidian languages do not owe their origin to the same people who produced the Neolithic culture there....... the several regional cultures ... had throughout a degree of interaction and probably originally a common language family, Dravidian...... the settlements of the third - second millennia appear to be ancestral to those which we encounter there from the beginnings of history onwards. (p. 353)
43. peruṅkaṟpaṭi nākarikam tamiḻnāṭṭiliruntē vaṭakkē takkāṇattiṟkup paraviyatu eṉṟu tolliyalaṟiñar A. cattiyamūrtti 2652007 intu nāḷitaḻil terivittuḷḷār.
(a) caṅkakālat tamiḻaka varalāṟu (ki.mu. 300 - ki.pi. 300)
43. tamiḻaka varalāṟṟil caṅka kālam potuvāka ki.mu. 300 mutal ki.pi. 300 vari eṉap perumpālāṉa varalāṟṟaṟiñar iṉṟu ēṟkiṉṟaṉar (cīṉivācar caṅka ilakkiya kālamāka nirṇa yitta kipi.1 - 600 kāla varampu iṉṟu ēṟkappaṭuvatu illi ) eṭṭuttoki - pattuppāṭṭu nūlkaḷil kālamum tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷa pāṭalkaḷ inta aṟunūṟu āṇṭukāla aḷavukkuḷ palvēṟu kālaṅkaḷic cārntavi. ki.mu. 300kku muṟpaṭṭatāṉa tolkāppiyam tamiḻaka mūvēntarip paṟṟik kūṟuvatu:
"vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ” 
"pōnti vēmpē ār eṉa urūum
māperum tāṉiyar malinta pūvum" 
cēracōḻapāṇṭiyar ākiya mūvēntarkaḷ maṟṟum kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ āṭciyiṉ teṉ elli kumari muṉi; vaṭa elli mēṟkuk kaṭaliliruntu kiḻakkuk kaṭal vari vēṅkaṭa maliyi aṭuttu vaṭapāl ceṉṟatu eṉalām.
44. cantira kupta meḷariyar (kimu. 324 - 300) kālattil kirēkka celyūkas nikēṭar tūtaṉāka irunta mekastaṉis pāṇṭiya nāṭṭi herāklas (= civaṉ?) makaḷ ''pāṇṭiya'' āṇṭu vantāḷ eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. pāṇṭi nāṭṭu ūrkaḷ 365 eṉṟum, ovvoru nāḷum araṇmaṉit tēvikaḷi ōr ūr niṟivēṟṟiyatākavum mekastaṉis iṉṉoru cey tiyik kūṟukiṟār. 1500 āṇṭu kaḻittu perumpaṟṟap puliyūr nampi kipi. 12 nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutiya tiruviḷiyāṭal purāṇattil pāṇṭiyaṉ makaḷ taṭātaki civaṉi maṇanta kati varukiṟatu. itupōṉṟa oru toṉmakkati 1500 āṇṭu kaḷāka vaḻaṅki vanta teṉka. cilappatikāram maturik kāṇṭat til āycci mātari "pāṇṭiyaṉ kōyilil ney muṟi namakku iṉṟu ām eṉṟu kūṟuvatu mekastaṉis coṉṉa iraṇ ṭāvatu ceytiyi niṉivūṭṭukiṟatu.
45. ilaṅkiyi ki.mu. 483-445l āṇṭa maṉṉaṉ vijayaṉ pāṇṭi araca kumāriyi maṇantatāka makāvamcam kūṟukiṟatu. aṉurātapurattil ki.mu. 307-247l ciṅkaḷa maṉṉaṉ muṭṭa civaṉ āṇṭa poḻutu teṉkiḻakku ilaṅkip pakutiyāṉa urō kaṇa nāṭṭil makamatti talinakarākak koṇṭu "majji marājaṉ' (macca makārājaṉ) eṉṟaḻikkappaṭṭa pāṇṭiya kulattavar cilar āṇṭatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
46. acōkaṉ (ki.mu. 273-236) cetukkiya 2vatu, 13vatu pāṟik kalveṭṭukaḷil tamiḻ vēntar kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.

''yē ca anta cōṭa, pāṇṭiya, catiyaputa, 
kēraḷapu tō, tāmpa paṉṉi, antiyōkō
nāma yōṉa rājā" 
''catiyaputō" eṉṟatu caṅka kāla atiyamāṉ paramparik kuṟunila maṉṉarik kuṟittatu. tirukkōyilūr arukē 15 kal tolivil aimpiyil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa kalveṭṭu oṉṟiṉ vācakam : 'catiyaputō atiyamāṉ neṭumāṉ añci ītta pāḷi" eṉpatākum. māmūlaṉār eṭṭuttokip pāṭalkaḷ mōriyariyum nantariyum cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. mōkūr mītu paṭiyeṭutta mōriyar taṅkaḷ tērkaḷic celutta malip pāṟikaḷi veṭṭi nīkki pāti ēṟpaṭuttiyati māmūlaṉār akanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷ 251,281 maṟṟum ēṉiya pulavar pāṭalkaḷ akam 69, puṟam 175 kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. innikaḻcci pintucāraṉ (kimu 298 - 273) kālattil nikaḻntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpar kē.kē. piḷḷiyum ci. irāmaccantiraṉum. (īṇṭu kuṟikkappaṭṭa mōriyar ''piṟkāla mōriyarē' eṉṟa cīṉivācar karuttu ēṟkappaṭuvatilli ) akam 265 māmūlaṉār pāṭalil,
palpukaḻ niṟinta velpōrnantar 
cīrmiku pāṭalik kuḻīi, kaṅki
nīrmutal karanta nitiyatti 
tēṭit talivaṉ ceṉṟuḷḷāṉō eṉap piriviṭi vēṟu paṭṭa talimakaḷ āṟṟāmi mītūrat tōḻiyiṭam col kiṟāḷ.
47. ki.mu. 2m nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyil irunta kaliṅka maṉṉaṉ kāravēlaṉiṉ hātikumpā (yāṉikkuki) kal veṭṭil kaliṅka nāṭṭukku ūṟuviḷitta, 13 āṇṭukaḷ ceyalpaṭṭu vanta tiramira ( tamiḻ ) (vāṇikak) kūṭṭamippi avaṉ veṉṟatākak kūṟukiṟatu.
48. ki.mu. 4-3m nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ cārnta tamiḻik kal veṭṭukaḷil akkālattil irunta paṇṭit tamiḻ maṉṉar, avarkaḷuṭiya ciṟṟaracar / atikārikaḷ ēṉiyōr peyarkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

(i) māṅkuḷam (ariṭṭāpaṭṭi maturi mīṉāṭcipuram arukē ARE 460/1906"kaṇinanti āciriyarkku aṅkē ēmam ītta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ paṉavaṉ kaṭalaṉ vaḻuti koṭupitta paḷḷi'
(ii) āṟunāṭṭārmali (pukaḷūr arukē), vēlāyutam pāḷiyum karūr tālukā ARE 34 mutaliyavi / 1927-28
(ācīvaka/camaṇa muṉivaṉ) "ceṅkāyapaṉ uṟiya kō ataṉ cēralirum poṟi makaṉ peruṅkaṭuṅkōṉ makaṉ iḷaṅkaṭuṅkō āka aṟutta kal iṅkuk kuṟitta cēra maṉṉar mūvarum patiṟṟuppattu 7,8,9 pattukkaḷiṉ talivarkaḷ eṉṟu ai. makātēvaṉ karutukiṟār.
(iii) eṭakkal, vayaṉāṭu māvaṭṭam, kēraḷā ; iṅku oru kukiyil 1996 pepravariyil ai makātēvaṉ paṭitta kalveṭṭu (mutalil 1887l lōkaṉ eḻutiya "malapār māṉuval" - il ikkalveṭṭup paṟṟi kūṟappaṭṭu iruntatu)
'kaṭummi puta cēra" = kaṭum māṉ cēraṉ 
'kōvātāṉ'' = kō ataṉ
(iv) tēṉi māvaṭṭam āṇṭippaṭṭi vaṭṭam viki yāṟṟiṉ teṉ kariyil pulimāṉ kōmpiyil 2006 mārc mātam kaṇṭu piṭitta ki.mu. 300kku muntiya mūṉṟu naṭukaṟkaḷil uḷḷa kalveṭṭukaḷ (kē. rājaṉ: 2007)
kalpēṭu tīyaṉ antavaṉ kūṭal ūrākōḷ 
aṉ ūr ataṉ ṉ aṉ kal
vēḷ ūr avvaṉ patavaṉ 
itē tamiḻi pōṉṟa eḻuttup pāṉip poṟippukaḷ pala, ki.mu. 600 - 500 cārntavi, ilaṅki aṉurātapurattilum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. (teraṉiyākala, es.yū. 2004)
49. caṅkakālap pāṇṭiya, cōḻa, cēra maṉṉar veḷiyiṭṭa kācukaḷ paṟṟi vivaraṅkaḷi kācu iyal āyvaṟiñar ār. kiruṣṇamūrtti tamatu caṅkakālak kācukaḷ (1997) nūlil iruntu aṟiyalām. ki.mu. 200liruntu im mūvarum veḷiyiṭṭa muttirik kācukaḷum, muttirikkācup pāṇiyip piṉpaṟṟi veḷiyiṭṭaṉavum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. 'peruvaḻuti' peyar poṟitta pāṇṭiya maṉṉar kācukaḷ ki.mu. 200 cārntavi. cēra maṉṉarkaḷ mākkōti, kuṭṭuvaṉ kōti peyar poṟitta kācukaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.
50. caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭum mūvēntarkaḷ ki.mu. 300 mutal nāṉkintu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil āṇṭavarkaḷ. (avarkaḷ aṉivarum ki.pi. 3-4 nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukkup piṟpaṭṭavarkaḷ eṉṟa cīṉivācar muṭivu iṉṟu ēṟkappaṭuvatilli eṉiṉum, avarkaḷip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ innūl iyalkaḷ 20-23, 25-26l tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. (avarkaḷ kāla variciyi caṅka ilakkiyac ceytikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil nirṇayikka kē.eṉ. civarācapiḷḷi (1932) mutalil muyaṉṟār. avar vaḻiyil mēlum kaṭumiyāka āḻamāka (1932kkup piṉṉar kiṭṭiya ceytikaḷiyum payaṉpaṭutti ) vi.pi. puruṣōttam 1989 il caṅkakāla maṉṉar kālanili varalāṟu eṉṉum nūli veḷiyiṭṭār. am maṉṉarkaḷ kimu 375 mutal ki.mu. 25 vari avar varicippaṭuttiyuḷḷār. caṅkappāṭalkaḷiṉ kāla variyaṟi paṟṟiya evā villaṉ (2002) kaṭṭuriyum paṭikkat takkatākum. ittuṟiyil aṟiñar mēlum muyaṉṟāl nāḷaṭi vil mēlum teḷivu kiṭikkalām.
51. caṅka kāla cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭu ovvoṉṟilum oru vēntaṉ maṭṭumē eṉṟa nili iṉṟi vevvēṟu pakutikaḷukku av vēntaṉ talimikku (muḻumiyākavō, ōraḷavukkō, peyaraḷavukkō) kaṭṭuppaṭṭu avvēntaṉ uṭaṉ piṟappu, uṟaviṉar ākiya "ciṟuvēntarum āṇṭu vantirukka vēṇṭum; eṉavētāṉ caṅka ilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷ ērāḷa māṉa vēntar peyarkaḷik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu nīlakaṇṭa cāstiri, eṉ. cuppiramaṇiyaṉ ākiyōr karutukiṉṟaṉar.
kū tamiḻar vāṇikam
52. innūliṉ iyalkaḷ 3,7,10,14,18 ākiya aintil ki.mu 4000liruntu kipi. 500 muṭiyavuḷḷa tamiḻaka ayal nāṭṭu vaṇikam cārnta pala ceytikaḷiyum cīṉivācar mika virivākat tan tuḷḷār. aṇmik kālac ceytikaḷ cilavaṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭāl pōtum :

(ii) ekiptil kuvecir - al - kātim eṉṉum iṭattil 1979. 80l kiṭitta ki.pi. 2m nūṟṟāṇṭic cārnta pāṉi ōṭukaḷil tamiḻi eḻuttukaḷil : kaṇaṉ (= kaṇṇa ṉ) ; cātaṉ  (= cāttaṉ, perṉikē tuṟimukattil kiṭitta kipi. 40 cārnta pāṉi ōṭṭil "kōṟpūmāṉ' eṉṟa poṟippu.
(iii) pēriyāṟu kaṭalil kalakkum iṭattiluḷḷa muciṟiyil vāḻnta tamiḻ vaṇikaṉum nil āṟu kaṭalil kalakkum alek cāntiriyā nakara kirēkka vaṇikaṉum, kipi150l ceyta vaṇika oppantam oṉṟu (pēpiracil kirīk moḻiyil eḻutiyatu) viyaṉṉā aruṅkāṭciyakattil 1985 il kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
(iv) kēraḷa erṇākuḷam māvaṭṭattil pēriyāṟṟiṉ teṉkariyil paṭṭaṉam eṉa iṉṟu aḻikkappaṭum iṭat til paṇṭiya muciṟiyiṉ taṭayaṅkaḷ, tamiḻp poṟippukaḷ koṇṭa pāṉi ōṭukaḷ, rōm nāṭṭu āmpōrā (matuc cāṭikaḷ) caṅka kālac cērar nāṇayam pōṉṟavi 2005 akaḻ vāyvil kiṭṭiyuḷḷaṉa. (vi.celvakumār kaṭṭuri: IUDL XXXV2, cūṉ 2006)

''tuṟa o" (= tuṟavōṉ) eṉṟa kipi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭu cārnta tamiḻp poṟippu koṇṭa pāṉi ōṭu oṉṟu tāylāntil kiṭittuḷḷatu. (intu: 1672007)
ka0. caṅka kālattil tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ motta makkaḷ toki matippīṭu :
53. kipi. 2001l ulaka motta makkaḷ toki 00 kōṭi (intiyā 100 kōṭi uṭpaṭa). intiyar 100 kōṭiyiṉaril tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉar 6 kōṭi. caṅkakāla araciyal, poruḷātāra, camūka, paṇpāṭṭu nilimikaḷip paṟṟi varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ avaravar cārntuḷḷa koḷkiyi oṭṭip palvēṟu nilimikaḷi tam viruppu veṟuppukkēṟpa uṉṉittu eḻutumpoḻutu mukkiyamākak karuttil koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatu eṉṉa? antak kālak kaṭṭattil, tamiḻaka motta makkaḷ tokiyē cila pala laṭcam aḷavi lēyē iruntirukkum eṉpatē atuvākum. paṇṭi intiyāviṉ peṅ kuyiṉ varalāṟu (2002) eṉṉum nūlil pākistāṉ, vaṅkāḷa tēcam uḷḷiṭṭa 1947kku muntiya intiyap parappiṉ motta makkaḷ tokiyip piṉvarumāṟu rōmilā tapār matip piṭṭuḷḷār.

54. kōlāmpūril 1966 ēpralil naṭanta mutal ulakat tamiḻārāyccik karuttaraṅku mānāṭṭukku kaṭṭurit tokuppu 1969 ēppiralil veḷivantatu. akkaṭṭuriyil oṉṟu emī. māṇikkavācakam piḷḷi eḻutiya 'caṅka kālattil tamiḻaka makkaḷ toki' eṉpatākum. taṟpōtiya kēraḷam caṅka kālattil tamiḻakattiṉ oru pakutiyē. 191 ceṉcaspaṭi taṟ pōtiya tamiḻnāṭu + kēraḷam motta makkaḷ toki 2.80 kōṭiyāka (2.01 + 079) iruntatu. (kipi 1l motta ulaka makkaḷ tokiyi 20 kōṭiyākak koṇṭu) 1911 tamiḻnāṭu + kēraḷā makkaḷ toki 2.80 kōṭiyip paṭippaṭi yākak kuṟittuk kaṇakkiṭṭu (caṅkakāla ) tamiḻaka makkaḷ tokiyip piṉ varumāṟu kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷār māṇikkavācakam : 

kipi.1 		36 laṭcam 
ki.pi. 300 		50 laṭca m 
ki.mu. 300 il tamiḻaka makkaḷ toki 36 laṭcattukkum kuṟivāka iruntirukkum. āka ki.mu. 300 mutal ki.pi. 300 muṭiya aṟunūṟu āṇṭukaḷumē. aṉṟiya tamiḻaka (vēṅkaṭattiṟku teṟkē teṉṉintiyā) makkaḷ toki entak kāla kaṭṭat tilum cumār 25 laṭcattukkum 50 laṭcattukkum iṭippaṭṭa oru eṇṇikkiyil tāṉ iruntirukkum. atāvatu iṉṟiya ceṉṉi nakara makkaḷ toki 40 laṭcattukku koñcam kūṭak kuṟiya irukkum. iṉṟiya tamiḻakap parappiṉ aṉṟiya makkaḷ toki 30 laṭcam eṉka. motta makkaḷ toki caṅkakālattil avvāṟu mikak kuṟintatāka irunta tuṭaṉ pōkkuvarattu vacatikaḷum mika mikak kuṟivāka irun tirukkum.
55. eṉavē varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ eccarikkiyuṭaṉ akkāla camūka maṟṟum piṟa nilimikaḷip paṟṟi eṇṇip pārttu eḻututal nalam. 1992l taṉatu ulakavaralāṟu (peli kaṉ veḷiyīṭu ) nūlil jē.em. rāparṭs viṭutta eccarikki ''paḻaṅkālattil irunta airōppiya makkaḷ aṉṟāṭa vāḻkki camūka uṟavukaḷ paṟṟiyellām potuvāṉa āṉāl apatta māṉa karuttōṭṭaṅkaḷik kūṟuvatu eḷitu eṉpatākum. atu caṅkakālat tamiḻarkkum poruntuvatākum. kāraṇam vil & ēriyal ṭurāṇṭ kūṟiyatu pōla "varalāṟṟil perumpakuti uṉṉippu vēli, mīti viruppu veṟuppiṉpaṭiyāṉa kūṟṟu ('most history is guessing and the rest is prejudice) "iṭatucāri " / ''valatu cāri' / ''naṭunili" pōṉṟa palvēṟu pōrvikaḷil varalāṟu eḻutupavarkaḷ palar ("Committed historians") tattam paṟṟu kāraṇamāka oru kōṇattil, oru niliyil, oru toliviliruntu (kāmālip pārvi pōṉṟu orē nōkkil pārttu tām eḻutuvatē varalāṟu ; ēṉiyōr eḻutum varalāṟukaḷ caṭṭavirōtam, nērmiyaṟṟatu, tavaṟu eṉṟu karutum pōkku tavirkkappaṭa vēṇṭiyatu ākum. muṉ iraṇṭu pakutikaḷiyum (89) aṭuttuvarum iraṇṭu pakutikaḷi yum paṭikkum poḻutu itaṉik karuttil koḷḷutal nalamākum.
kaka. camutāyamum paṇpāṭum
56. iṉṟiyat tamiḻ nāṭṭup parappiṉ caṅkakāla makkaḷ toki 30 laṭcattukku mēl iruntirukkātu eṉṟu kaṇ ṭōm. nāṭṭiṉ perumpakuti kāṉakamāka iruntirukkum; pōkkuvarattu vacatikaḷ mikamikak kuṟivu. aintiṇikaḷilum irunta makkaḷ perumpālum iṉak kuḻuviṉarākavē irun tiruppar. toḻil aṭippaṭiyil vēṟupaṭṭa inta iṉak kuḻukkaḷ perumpālum akamaṇak kuḻukkaḷē. aintiṇi oḻukkam paṟṟi innūl iyalkaḷ 1, 5l cīṉivācar viḷakki yuḷḷār. akkālattil cātiyō varṇā ciramō iruntiruk kātu. (iṉṟum tirāviṭa moḻi pēcum iṉak kuḻuviṉarāṉa kantar, oravōṉ pōṉṟavariṭam cāti illi ) nāl varuṇam paṟṟik kūṟum tol poruḷ, marapiyal cūttiraṅkaḷ 71 - 85 iṭiccerukal eṉpati aṉivarum ēṟkiṉṟaṉar. āriyat tākkam tamiḻnāṭṭil ki.mu. 4m nūṟṟāṇṭi oṭṭiyē metuvākat toṭaṅki yirukkum eṉpar kē.kē.piḷḷi (1968) camaṇar, puttar, pirāmaṇa matattiṉar eṉṟa variciyil it tākkam iruntirukkum eṉkiṟār. pirāmaṇar tamatu varuṇā ciramak koḷkiyit taṉṉala nōkkuṭaṉ virakākap payaṉ paṭutti tirāviṭa iṉakkuḻuc camūkatti vētiyiyal māṟṟam aṭiyac ceytu, cātikaḷiyum cāti vēṟṟumikaḷiyum tīṇṭāmik koṭumikaḷiyum uruvākkiṉar eṉpar slē ṭar (1924) "The Aryans found a system resembling caste already in force among the Dravidian inhabitants and they adapted and modified it to suit their Own purposes" tamiḻakattil ivva kiyil āriyat tākkam ēṟpaṭṭa kālam ki.pi. 600kkup piṉṉarttāṉ irukka vēṇṭum. tirukkuṟaḷum yāṇṭum "oru kulattukkoru nīti" kūṟavilli .
57. caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil "antaṇar' eṉac cuṭṭappaṭu vōr aṉivarum āriyap pirāmaṇar alla. vēḷvi paṟṟiya ceytikaḷum cila viṭaṅkaḷil vanta pōtilum avaṟṟic ceyta aṉivarum vaṭanāṭṭiliruntu vanta āriyar eṉṟu karuta iṭamilli ; akkālattilum cari piṟkālattilum cari tamiḻ makkaḷ cilarum pūṇūl aṇintum vētac caṭaṅkukaḷi kaṭippiṭittum pirāmaṇarāka māṟiyiruppar eṉpar eṉ. cupramaṇiyam (1998). The other groups which go by the local names were in all probability groups of Tamil wise men and religious folk who become converts to Brahminism by sporting the thread and assuming Brahminical ways). rik vēta kālattilēyē cintu veḷit tamiḻ nākarika aṟiñar pirāmaṇarkaḷāka, riṣikaḷāka āriyaruṭaṉ kalantu viṭṭaṉar eṉpati mēlē kaṇṭōm.
58. paḻaṅkāla (ki.mu. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu toṭak kattiliruntu tamiḻ - pirāmi (tamiḻik kalveṭṭukkaḷ 89aiyum toṭakkakāla vaṭṭeḻuttuk kalveṭṭukaḷ 21aiyum tamatu "toṭakkakāla tamiḻk kalveṭṭukkaḷ āti mutal ki.pi. 6m nūṟṟāṇṭu muṭiya (2003) ām āṇṭiya nūlil cemmi yākap patippitta aṟiñar ai.makātēvaṉ oru mukkiyamāṉa karutti veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. tamiḻnāṭṭil ikkāla kaṭṭattic cārnta kalveṭṭukkaḷ, ērāḷamāṉa pāṉi ōṭṭup poṟippukaḷ, kācukaḷ, muttirikaḷ ākiyavi yellām tamiḻil uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻakap potu makkaḷiṭam paravalāka eḻuttaṟivu iruntataṟku itu cāṉṟu eṉkiṟār avar. (tamiḻnāṭṭukku vaṭakkil uḷḷa teṉṉintiyap pakuti yilō veṉṟāl avi appakutip potu makkaḷ pēciya teluṅku / kaṉṉaṭa moḻikaḷil allāmal appakuti mēṭṭukkuṭiyiṉarukku maṭṭum terinta pirākiruta moḻiyil uḷḷaṉa. appakutip potumakkaḷ eḻuttaṟivu peṟṟilar eṉpatutāṉ kāraṇam.) avvaṭa pakutikaḷil āṭci naṭattiya meḷariyar, cātavākaṉar, ikṣvākukaḷ, kaṭampar, cālaṅ kāyaṉar, viṣṇukuṇṭiṉ, pallavar ākiyōr makkaḷ moḻi kaḷilaṉṟi pirākirutattil āṭci naṭattiṉār. tamiḻaka maṉṉarō veṉil tamiḻil āṭci naṭattiṉar. ataṉāltāṉ tamiḻaka makkaḷiṭam caṅka kālattil eḻuttaṟivu ciṟantiruntatu. caṅka ilakkiyap pulavar 473 pērkaḷuḷ 30 pēr peṇṭir (kuṟamakaḷ, kuyattiyar uṭpaṭa).
59. caṅka kālattilēyē ici, naṭaṉam, ōviyam ākiya kalikaḷ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉa. iṉṟiya intiya ici āriyar tamiḻariṭamiruntu kaṟṟatē eṉṟa kōsvāmi (1957, karutti mēlē 23m pakutiyil kuṟittōm. avar kūṟṟil orupakuti piṉvarumāṟu : "iciyaṟivi āriyarallātavarkaḷiṭam āriyar kaṭaṉ peṟṟati maṟippataṟkākavē, cāma vētattiliruntu intiya ici piṟantatu eṉa avarkaḷ kati puṉintaṉar. "By this fiction that Saman is the source of all Indian music alone it was possible to forget conveniently the debt the Aryans owed to the non - Aryans for their musical knowledge and practice".ēḻici eḻuttiṉ peyarukku cēntaṉ tivākaram nikaṇṭil iraṇṭu nūṟpākkaḷ uḷ.

pavvum tavvum nivvum eṉṟivi 
ēḻum avaṟṟiṉ eḻuttē yākum 
'ā ī ū ē aiō ōḷa eṉṉum
ivvēḻ eḻuttum ēḻicikkuriya" 
itil mutalāvatu nūṟpā piṟkāla iṭic cerukal (atuvum aḷavi yiyal niyati teriyāmal iraṇṭāvataṟku muṉṉarc cerukiyuḷḷaṉar!) eṉpar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār (1993). iṉṟu karunāṭaka ici eṉappeyar koṇṭu ulavuvatu tamiḻiciyē. mukamatiyar 1565kkup piṉṉar teṉṉāṭṭiṟ pukunta pōtu (vicaya nakarak) karunāṭaka maṉṉar talimiyā yiruntatālum tamiḻnāṭṭilum karunaṭa maṉṉar talimi iruntatālum karunāṭakac caṅkītam eṉṟum aḻittaṉar.
60. jārj lūcarṉ hārṭ tamatu "paḻantamiḻc ceyyuḷ kaḷ: avi tōṉṟiya cūḻalum, avaṟṟi aṭiyoṟṟiya camskirutap paṭippukaḷum" (1975) - nūlil kūṟuvatu, varumāṟu: 
''pāṇar tamiḻariṭiyē toṉṟu toṭṭu iruntu varupavar; camutāyattil tāḻnta niliyil iruntavar; eṉavē paṇ aṭippaṭiyil aminta teṉṉintiyat toṉmi ici vaṭavariṭamiruntu kaṭaṉ peṟṟatāka irukka iyalavē iyalātu. ''paṇ" eṉṉum colliliruntu "pāṇaṉ" eṉṉum col tōṉṟiyatu. māṟākap piṟkāla intiya iciyiṉ rākaṅkaḷ (avi paṇkaḷiṉ urumāṟṟaṅkaḷē) teṉṉintiyat tol ici yiliruntu uruvāyiṉa eṉpatē cari. takkāṇap peruṅkaṟkāla nākarikam cārnta vāymoḻi ilakkiyamāṉatu pāṇarkaḷ maṟṟum eḻuttaṟivu peṟāta nāṭṭuppuṟap pāṭakar kaḷiṭiyē uruvākik ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭi oṭṭi makārāṣṭirap pirākirutattil paravi, aṅkiruntu ivvilak kiyattiṉ tākkam camaskirutak kaviñarkaḷiṭam ēṟpaṭṭataṉ kāraṇattāltāṉ camaskirutattil taramāṉa kāviyac cey yuḷilakkiyam tōṉṟiyatu eṉpati ipputtakattiṉ iraṇ ṭām pakutiyil kāṇalām. ikkāla kaṭṭattil allatu itaṟkuc caṟṟu muṉṉart teṉ intiya icikkaliyum vaṭa intiyāvil paraviyirukka vēṇṭum eṉa eṉakkut tōṉṟu kiṟatu. itu paṟṟi virivāṉa , āḻnta āyvukaḷ ituvari ceyyappaṭavilliyāki yāl itit tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa muṭivu eṉṟu iṉṟiya niliyil kūṟa iyalātu. ikkaruttiṉ aṭippaṭiyil viyakkattakkatum maṉaniṟivu taruvatumāṉa oru ceyti veḷivarukiṟatu. atāvatu teṉ intiyac cātiyiṉaril tāḻniliyil irunta pāṇariṭiyē iruntutāṉ intiyā viṉ iciyum pāṭalkaḷum perumaḷavu uruvāyiṉa eṉṉum ceytiyē atu.''
61. cīr - cīri - cīrmi ; cīraṉ - kīraṉ; cīrtti - kīrttaṉi ; āka tamiḻc cīrttaṉi kīrttaṉiyākiyatu. ik kīrttaṉi vaṭivam (atāvatu pallavi, aṉupallavi, caraṇam) caṅka kālattilē uruvāṉatē yeṉpatiyum atu piṟkālattu purantaratācarō, tāḷḷapākkam aṉṉamācāryarō uruvāk kiṉareṉpatu piḻiyeṉṟum mu. aruṇācalam (1980) niṟuvi yuḷḷār.
62. iciyoṭu (āṭalum naṭaṉamum) kūttum caṅka kālattilēyē ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉa. akkālattiyoṭṭi vanta cilappatikārattil akkalikaḷiṉ uccaniliyik kāṇa lām; aṭiyārkku nallār uri akkali nuṭpaṅkaḷi viḷakkukiṟatu. tamiḻ āṭaṟkali 1925 vari ciṉṉamēḷam / catirkkaccēri eṉṟē aḻikkappaṭṭatu. appeyari māṟṟi ''parata nāṭṭiyam' eṉap putup peyariṭṭavar ī. kiruṣṇa aiyar. tamiḻ āṭaṟkali ''paratar peyaril vaḻaṅkum nāṭya cāstirattil iruntu piṟantatu eṉak kūṟuvatu pitaṟṟal eṉpati puraṭcitācaṉ tam nūlkaḷ palavaṟṟil niṟuviyuḷḷār.
63. caṅka nūlkaḷil kāṇum kaṭṭaṭakkali cintuveḷi nākarikak kaṭṭaṭakkaliyiṉ toṭarcciyē. eṭi aḷavukaḷ, ciṟṟilakka muṟi ; 4 cemī. (= 3 aṅkulam) kōl ; 2 % X4 = 17 ākum. 11, 22, 33 eṉṟavāṟu 11ṉ maṭaṅkāka varum aḷavu kaḷ, pōṉṟavi yellām cintuveḷik kālattiliruntu toṭarntu varupaviyē (es. caṇmukam 1980 ; ku. vēṅkaṭācalam 1983, teyva nāyakam 2002). vaṭa intiyat tol tamiḻa cintu veḷi makkaḷiṭamiruntē āriyar piṟa kalikaḷip pōla kaṭṭaṭak kali yiyum kaṟṟuk koṇṭirukka vēṇṭum. am makkaḷuṭiya kōṭṭi kaḷiyum nakaraṅkaḷiyum paṟṟi rik vētattil kuṟippukaḷ pala. piṟkāla makāpāratamum yutiṣ ṭiraṉukku ciṟanta araṇmaṉiyi oru tāṉavaṉē (acuraṉē) kaṭṭik koṭuttatākak kūṟukiṟatu. ōviyakkaliyum ciṟantu iruntatic caṅka ilakkiyam kāṭṭukiṟatu. maṇimēkali "ōviyac cennūl" paṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.
ku camayam, meyyiyal
64. tolkāppiyam kūṟum aintiṇik kaṭavuḷar mullikku māyōṉ, kuṟiñcikku cēyōṉ, marutattukku vēntaṉ, neytalukku varuṇaṉ āvar. vēntaṉukkum varuṇa ṉukkum paḻantamiḻp peyarkaḷ cēṇōṉ, kaṭalōṉ eṉṟu iruntirukkalām eṉpar cīṉivācar. (ceṉṉip pakuti mīṉavar kaṭalammiyi vaḻipaṭukiṉṟaṉar; maliyāḷak kariyilum kaṭa lammā vaṇakkam uṇṭu. eṉavē kaṭalōṉ eṉṉum peyar varuṇaṉ eṉṟu māṟṟappaṭṭirukkalām eṉpar cuvelepilum) māyōṉ piṟkālattil māl (atāvatu viṣṇuviṉ avatāra māṉa kiruṣṇaṉ) ākak karutappaṭṭāṉ. vaṭa intiyat tamiḻiyak kaṭavuḷ oṉṟu viṇ - viṣṇu eṉṟu māṟṟap paṭṭirukkalām eṉpar cīṉivācar, pirislaski (Przluzki), el.vi. irāmacāmi aiyar pōṉṟavarkaḷ. caṅka ilakkiyam 67 - 69 iṭaṅkaḷil murukaṉic cuṭṭukiṟatu; 27 iṭaṅkaḷil vēlaṉic (vēli uṭiyavaṉ = murukaṉ) cuṭṭukiṟatu. tamiḻak kaṭavuḷ oṉṟiṉ iruvēṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷē murukaṉum civaṉum eṉpar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ. piṉṉar civaṉ eṉa aḻikkappaṭṭa rik vēta rutraṉ āriyarallātāruṭiya kaṭavuḷē eṉṟu cintu veḷi akaḻ vāyvukku muṉṉarē aṟiñar muṭivu ceytu viṭṭaṉar. avvakaḻvāyvil kiṭṭiya pacupati muttiri, 'yōki' uruvacciṟpam mēlum pala cāṉṟukaḷik kaṇṭa car jāṉ mārṣal ulakil mikat toṉmiyāṉatum iṉṟum nilavivaruvatumāṉa vaḻipāṭu civaṉ vaḻipāṭē eṉpār. (taṇṭēkarum 1979 nūlil ikkaruttiṉarē) caṅka ilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷ "civaṉ " peyarik kuṟippiṭṭila ; avaṉi viḷakkum toṭarkaḷ maṭṭumē 'ālamar celvaṉ"; "mukkaṭ celvaṉ'' pōṉṟavi uḷḷaṉa.
65. cintuveḷi nākarikattiliruntu iṉṟu varit tāyt teyva vaḻipāṭum tirāviṭariṭam ciṟappāka uḷḷatu koṟ ṟavi, paḻiyōḷ (cēyōṉiṉ tāy - perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭi ; 458) kāṉamar celvi, kāṭukiḻāḷ, kaṭalkeḻu celvi, ellammaṉ/elliyammaṉ (piṉṉar rēṇukā eṉa camas kirutap peyar peṟṟāḷ ) eṉap pala peyarkaḷil tāyt teyvam vaḻipaṭappaṭukiṟatu. (piel. cāmi 1975).
66. camaṇa, putta, ācīvaka camayaṅkaḷum āriyarukkum muntiya vaṭanāṭṭut tamiḻiya meyyiyaliṉ veḷippāṭukaḷē. (aṭutta patti kāṇka.) ācīvaka camayam tamiḻakattilēyē tōṉṟiya teṉpar ka. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ (2006). maturi nakkīrar puṟam 396l kuṟippiṭum "aṟap peyarc cāttaṉ maṟkali kōcālar āṉa mācāttaṉic cuṭṭuvatē eṉpar. ācīvaka camaya eccaṅkaḷ pala paravalākat tamiḻakattil iṉṟum uḷḷati avar viḷakku kiṟār.
67. iṉṟiya intu camayak kaṭavuḷarum vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷum muṟṟilum tamiḻiya (tirāviṭa) cārpuṭiyaviyē; āriyariṭamiruntu peṟṟavi alla eṉpati cīṉivācar iyal 8 mutaliyavaṟṟil niṟuvi yuḷḷār. maṉōṉmaṇīyam cuntaram piḷḷi, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ pōṉṟōrum ciṟanta mēṉāṭṭa ṟiñarum cīṉivācar karuttiyē koṇṭuḷḷaṉar. jē. em. nallacāmi piḷḷikku cuntarampiḷḷi , 19-12-1896il eḻutiya kaṭitattil ''aṟiyāmi kāraṇamāka 'āriya' meyyiyal, nākarikam eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭuvatu, aṭippaṭiyil tirāviṭar allatu tamiḻaruṭiyatu eṉpatē uṇmi .'' Most of what is ignorantly called Aryan philosophy and civilisation is literally Dravidian or Tamilian at bottom." Siddhantha Deepika , II - 5; Oct 1898 P.113) intu camayak kōṭpāṭukaḷil aṭippaṭiyākavum ciṟantaṉa vākavum uḷḷaviyellām vaṭanāṭṭil paravalāka 3000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ vāḻnta tamiḻ mēṉmakkaḷiṭamiruntu peṟappaṭṭavi eṉpatiyum, avaṟṟi āriyaruṭiyavi yākak koḷvatu aṟiyāmi eṉpatiyum, maṟimali aṭikaḷ tamatu 1903, 1923, 1930, 1941 nūlkaḷil niṟuviyuḷḷār. kā. cuppiramaṇiya piḷḷi ("tamiḻar camayam 1940), tēvanēyap pāvāṇar ("tamiḻar matam" "1972) ākiyōrukkum aṭikaḷ karuttu uṭaṉpāṭēyākum. āriya vētaṅkaḷiliruntu māṟu paṭṭa, avaṟṟukku muntiya 'tamiḻ nāṉmaṟikaḷ iruntaṉa veṉṉum kōṭpāṭṭiyum kā. cuppiramaṇiyar (1927) mutaliyōr koṇṭiruntaṉar. rik vēta kālattiliruntē (ki.mu. 1000) "kuṭi koli malinta āriya vēḷviyiṉ tīviṉip peṟṟiyuñ ciṟu teyva vaṇakkac ciṟumiyun tēṟṟi aṉpum aruḷum perukac ceyta tamiḻc cāṉṟōrum uḷar. avar aruḷic ceytaṉavē; muttī vēḷviyum uruttira vaḻipāṭuñ ciṟanteṭuttuk kūṟum irukku, ēcur, cāma, atarvaṇa vētappatikaṅkaḷ cila palavum; uṇmi vaḻā upaniṭataṅkaḷum, cāṅkiya , yōka, vicēṭika, niyāyika, vētānta cūttiraṅ kaḷum; maṟcam , vāyu mutalāṉa cila purāṇaṅkaḷum peḷaṭkaram, mirukēntiram mutalāṉa cila civākamaṅkaḷum piṟavumām" eṉpatu aṭikaḷ (1930) muṭivu. im muṭivukkāṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷāka aṭikaḷ tantuḷḷa palavaṟṟuḷ mutaṉmiyāṉa cila varumāṟu.
"irukku vētattiṟ "paratar" mutaliya peyar kaḷāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa paṇṭit tamiḻ naṉmakkaḷē ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, tī eṉṉum muttī vaṭivil vittu muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāṉa civa pirāṉi vaḻipaṭum nuṭpa muṟiyik kaṇṭōrāvar" (1930).
"vicuvāmittirar eṉṉum tamiḻaraca muṉi varāl ceytu cērkkappaṭṭa irukku vēta mūṉ ṟām maṇṭilattil uḷḷa kāyattiri mantira māṉatu ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattiṉ kaṇ muḷittu viḷaṅkum parkkaṉ eṉṉum peyaruṭiya civa pirāṉ mēṟṟāy viḷaṅkukiṟatu" (1923).
''kollā aṟatti mutaṉ mutaṟ kaṇṭaṟintu ataṟkēṟpat tamatu immi vāḻkkiyi naṭatti ṉavarkaḷ tamiḻaril mēṉ makkaḷāy irunta oru perumpakutiyārē ... maṟṟi makkaṭpiriviṉariṉ luḷḷa enta mēlōrum ivvaru maruntaṉṉa uṇmiyiṉik kaṇṭaṟintilar... vaṭak kirunta camaṇar, cākkiyar, cāṅkiyar, yōkar mutalāyiṉā ruṅkollā vaṟattiṉi viṭāppiṭiyāyk koṇṭu oḻukiṉarallarō veṉil; camaṇa cākkiyar mutalāṉa avarellām āriyar varu taṟku muṉṉamē imayamali variyiṟ paravi nāka rikattiṟ ciṟantu uyir vāḻnta vaṭanāṭṭut tamiḻmēṉ makkaḷiṉ marapil vantavarē" (1940). 
camaṇa, puttak karuttukkaḷ āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa vaṭa nāṭṭut tirāviṭa meyyiyalil piṟkāla vaḷarccikaḷē eṉpar car jāṉ mārṣalum (1934).
68. aṭikaḷiṉ mēṟcoṉṉa āyvuk karuttukkaḷiṉ karuvip piṉvarumāṟu kā. appātturiyār 1956il ("1800 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻakam" tamiḻākkattiṉ aṭik kuṟippil curuṅkak kuṟittār:
"tamiḻar paṇṭiyac camaya vāḻvu iṉṟiya pala camayaṅ kaḷiṉ uruvākā mūlak karu mutalit taṉṉiṭamākak koṇṭa vāḻvēyākum. atil ellāc camaya mūlamum kāṇalām. putta, camaṇa, civa, viṇavak karuttukkaḷ uruvākumuṉ, ac camayap peyarkaḷ vēṟuvēṟākat tamiḻar neṟiyiṉiṉṟu piriyumuṉ, āriyam iṭi niṉṟu tamiḻariṉ oru neṟiyip palavāṟu pirikkumuṉ, avvellā neṟikaḷukkum vaḻikāṭṭiya mika muṟpaṭṭa kālat tamiḻ aṟivar marapu cārnta aṟiñaruḷ oruvarē tiruvaḷḷuvar eṉpatu uyttuṇarat takka uṇmi yākum.
69. ipporuḷ kuṟittu mēlum cila mēṉāṭṭaṟiñar kūṟṟukkaḷum maṉattiṟ koḷḷattakkaṉa.
pirākkiṅṭaṉ (1981) : intiyāvil camaya vaḷarcciyil kuṟippiṭattakkatu oṉṟu uṇṭu. uyirkaḷ mīṇṭum mīṇṭum piṟaviyeṭukkum eṉpatu putta matattiṉ mukkiya koḷki. puttamatam tōṉṟiya kālattil tōṉṟiya camaṇamum ācī vakamum akkoḷkiyuṭiyaviyē. piṟavit taḷiyi liruntu viṭupaṭuvatu camayattiṉ kuṟikkōḷ eṉpatu am mataṅkaḷiṉ koḷki.
paḻiya upaniṭataṅkaḷil maṟupiṟavik koḷki putumi yāṉatākavum cilarukku maṭṭum terinta maṟiporuḷākavum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭu iruntatu. iruntum piṉṉar ak koḷki putta camaṇa camayaṅkaḷ vaḻiyāka virintu aṉivarālum piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭatu evvāṟu? enta makkaḷ, camūkattiṉa riṭam iruntu puttar tōṉṟiṉārō, avarkaḷiṭiyē ak koḷkiyāka avvētaṅkaḷ iruntu irukka vēṇṭum. eṉavē tāṉ atu virivil aṉittu makkaḷiṉ kōṭ pāṭāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
heṉric cimmar (Heinrich Zimmer) (1951) : vaṭa mēṟkiliruntu nuḻinta āriyarkaḷāl makatam mutaliya kīḻttici nāṭukaḷilirunta āriyarallāta mēṉmakkaḷ muḻumi yāka iṭam peyarntilar. attakiya mēṉmakkaḷ kuṭumpaṅkaḷ pala toṭarntu iruntaṉa. āriya āḷum kuṭumpaṅkaḷ valu viḻantu vīḻat toṭaṅkiya piṉ muntiya uḷḷūr āḷum kuṭumpaṅkaḷ mīṇṭum valuppeṟṟaṉar. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka cantirakupta meḷariyaṉ ittakiya kuṭumpattic cārntavaṉ. puttarum avvāṟē.
leviṉ (1986) : intu camayattil yāṇṭum kāṇappaṭum uruva vaḻipāṭu intiyāvil irunta āriyar allātār vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷil iruntē pirāmaṇīyattukku vantatu.
vālparṭ (1982) : mokañcotarō muttiriyil kāṇappaṭupavar pōṉṟa (āriyarkaḷukku muntiya intiya) yōkikaḷ vantēṟikaḷ moḻiyic cila kālattukkuḷ kaṟṟuk koṇṭu tamatu camaya maṟi poruṭkaḷi (vētap pāṭalkaḷi iyaṟṟiya) pirāmaṇarkaḷukkuk kaṟpikkat toṭaṅki viṭṭa ṉarō ?
vālparṭ (1991) : āriya nāṭōṭikaḷiṉ muraṭṭup paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, avarkaḷuṭiya kaṭumiyāṉa perum pōr kaḷ ākiyavaṟṟukkum ahimcikkum (uyirkaḷukku ūṟu ceyyāmi ) neṭuntolivu ākiyāl, yōkam, tāyt teyva vaḻipāṭu pōṉṟavaṟṟip pōla ahimciyum āriyar kaḷukkum muṟpaṭṭa (cintu veḷit tamiḻ makkaḷiṭamiruntē avarkaḷ peṟṟatāka nām karuta vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.
civaṉ vaḻipāṭu vaṭa intiyāvi viṭa teṉṉāṭṭil tāṉ makkaḷiṭam mutaṉmi peṟṟuḷḷatu. teṉṉāṭṭut tirāviṭa makkaḷ āriyarkaḷukku neṭuṅkālattukku muṉṉarē aṅku nilipeṟṟu iruntamiyi itu kāṭṭuvatākalām. tāyt teyva vaḻipāṭum teṉṉāṭṭil tāṉ mikuntu kāṇap paṭukiṟatu.
70. mēṟkaṇṭa palvēṟu camayaṅkaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ meyyiyal pōṉṟavi paṟṟiya uṇarvellām potu makkaḷil pattu viḻuk kāṭṭukkum kuṟivāṉavarkaḷiṭiyē tāṉ ōraḷavukku iruntirukkum. ēṉiya 90 viḻukkāṭṭiṉar nili vēṟu. ivvuṇmiyi kaṉakacapip piḷḷi (1904) aṉṟē kūṟiṉār. ellā nāṭukaḷilum, ellāk kālaṅkaḷilum (paṇṭum iṉṟum uḷḷatu pōl tamiḻariṭiyēyum ittakiya camayak kōṭpāṭukaḷukkum vekumakkaḷ naṭimuṟiyil kaṭip piṭikkum camaya nampikkikaḷ, caṭaṅkukaḷukkum perutta vēṟu pāṭu uṇṭu (As usual among all nations, ancient and modern, the philosophic doctrines of the Tamils were far apart from the popular beliefs and ceremonies). itē karutti hec.ār pēṭ tamatu tirunelvēli kecaṭṭiyaril (1917) piṉ varumāṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār:
''ūr tōṟum potumakkaḷ maṉappūrvamāka ūkkat tuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭum ērāḷamāṉa uḷḷurt teyvaṅkaḷip paṟṟi aṭuttu viḷakkappaṭukiṟatu. mikap perumpāṉmiyāṉa mak kaḷip poruttavarit taṅkaḷ aṉṟāṭa vāḻkki nikaḻccikaḷi nirṇayippavarkaḷum, (māntaṉ teyvamāka āṉāl eppaṭi naṭappāṉō appaṭi) ciṉam ēṟpaṭṭāl kēṭu viḷikkak kūṭiyavarkaḷum, pontiki ēṟpaṭṭāl kēṭu varāmal taṭuttum, vantāl nīkkiyum nalam ceyyak kūṭiyavarkaḷum āka avarkaḷ nampuvatu civaṉ, tirumāli alla; palvēṟu uruvaṅkaḷil vaḻipaṭappaṭum tāyt teymākiya kāḷi, maṟṟum uḷḷurt teyvaṅkaḷāṉa cuṭalimāṭaṉ maṟṟum atupōṉṟa "tuṭiyāṉa teyvaṅkaḷiyē"
“The accounts which will shortly be given of the hosts of lesser deities and of the enthusiasm with which they are worshipped may serve to show that the gods who for the mass of the people rule and direct their dily lives, who bring evil and can if willing remove it, who are intelligible beings - men on the grand scale are not Siva and Vishnu but Kali in her various forms, and (local gods like) Sudalimadan "and his horrid crew"
kaṅa. caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ
muccaṅkaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya piṉvaruvaṉavaṟṟi mutaṉ mutalil kipi. 10m nūṟṟāṇṭil iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷukku uri yeḻutiya (piṟkāla) nakkīrarum, avarukkup piṉṉar cilappatikārattukku uriyeḻutiya aṭiyārkku nallārum, kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar :

ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉiraṇṭu caṅkaṅkaḷ paṟṟiyavaṟṟi varalāṟ ṟāciriyar, varalāṟṟuṇarvu uṭiyavarkaḷ ēṟpatilli . (cīṉivācar iyal 16il mūṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya ceyti kaḷumē ātāramaṟṟa kaṟpaṉikaḷ (absurd inventions) eṉpār. ''mūṉṟām caṅkattip poruttavari avar kūṟṟu cariyalla. kīḻē kāṇka.)
72. perumpālāṉa varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷum piṟatuṟi aṟiñarkaḷum kiṟittuva ūḻit toṭakkattukkuc cila nūṟ ṟāṇṭukaḷ muṉṉum piṉṉum maturiyil tamiḻc caṅkam atiyotta ilakkiya pīṭam iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpati ēṟṟuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.
''maturiyil pāṇṭiya vēntar ātaravil cila kālam tamiḻppulavar amippu caṅkam oṉṟu ceyalpaṭṭu vantatu uṇmiyāṉa ceytiyāka irukkalām" - ka. a. nīlakaṇṭa cāttiri.
''tamiḻccaṅkam iruntatu eṉṟa marapuc ceyti iṭiviṭātu iruntu varukiṟatu. (intiyāvil ittakiya marapuc ceyti vēṟeṅkum illi.) tamiḻc cevvilakkiya (''caṅka") kālattil ilakkiyaṅkaḷi vaḷarttu, taram kaṇṭu ūkkuvitta oru amippu iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpati itu kāṭṭukiṟatu" - cēviyar es. taṉināyakam.
"caṅka ilakkiyap pāṭalkaḷil taṉippāṭal kaḷāyiṉum avaṟṟiṭiyē naṭi, coṟ ṟoki, yāppu, pāṭuporuḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟil oṟṟumi kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ilakkiya amippu cārnta taṉmi " "academism" eṉa itik kūṟalām. akātemi pōṉṟa ilakkiya amippu illāmal attakiya oṟṟumi ēṟpaṭṭirukkātu. ki.pi. 1ai oṭṭi maturiyil attakiya akātemi , atā vatu caṅkam iruntatu eṉa muṭivu ceyvataṟkāṉa pala ātāraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. - kē. vi. cuvelapil Tamil literature (1974) Otto HarrassOvitz. Wiesbaden. 
ikkarutti mēlum araṇ ceyvaṉa maturiyil (kūṭalil) caṅkam iruntatiyō allatu accaṅkattiṉ ceyal pāṭṭiyō kuṟikkum ilakkiyaṅkaḷum kalveṭṭukaḷum ākum. avi varumāṟu. 
"tamiḻ keḻu kūṭal' 						- puṟam 58:13

teṉmaturāpuram ceytum aṅkataṉil aruntamiḻ naṟ caṅkam irīit tamiḻ vaḷarttum - taḷavāypuram ceppēṭu (parāntaka vīranārāyaṇaṉ ki.pi. 9m nū)
"māpāratam tamiḻppaṭuttum maturāpuric caṅkam vitta tum" - periya ciṉṉamaṉūrc ceppēṭu (parāntaka vīra nārāyaṇaṉ makaṉ irāca cimmaṉ)
'	'tamiḻ keḻu kūṭal caṅkap palakiyil '' amarntirunta pulavar parampariyic cārnta eṭṭiccāttaṉip paṟṟiya kuṟippu. 9m nūṟṟāṇṭu kalveṭṭu (ARE 334/1929-30) erukkaṅkuṭi, cāttūr vaṭṭam.

eṉkiṟār) 
[mēṟkaṇṭa aṉittu nūlkaḷumē ki.pi. 1m nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṟpaṭṭavi eṉṟu cīṉivācar innūlil kūṟu vati iṉṟu yārum ēṟpatilli) 
cēviyar es taṉināyakam taṉatu 'iyaṟkiyum tamiḻc ceyyuḷum" (1996) eṉṉum nūlil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa piṉvarum karuttu mika mukkiyamāṉatu.
"tamiḻc caṅkam ceyyuḷ tamiḻakattilēyē karukkoṇṭu vaḷarntatu eṉpataṟkāṉa aṉittuc cāṉṟukaḷiyum koṇ ṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻ pēcunar veḷināṭṭiliruntu vantu iṅku kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ eṉṟō, varumpoḻutē vaḷarcciyaṭinta tamiḻ moḻi, ilakkiyatti uṭaṉ koṇṭu vantaṉar eṉṟō kūṟuvataṟkuc caṅka ilakkiyam iṭam taravilli eṉpatē itaṉ poruḷ."
74. mikap paḻiya kaḻaka nūlkaḷilēyē (tolkāppiyam, eṭṭuttoki nūlkaḷil aṭaṅkiya pāṭalkaḷil toṉmi cāṉṟavi) tamiḻ varivaṭivam, eḻuttumuṟi kuṟittum paṇpaṭṭa ilakkiyam kuṟittum teḷivāka, virivākak kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ai. makātēvaṉ (2003) karuttu, acōkaṉuṭiya tamiḻ - pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷiliruntu tamiḻ varivaṭivam uru vākkappaṭṭatu eṉpatākum. ikkaruttu ēṟkattakkatāka illi . kāraṇam mēṟcoṉṉa ki.mu. 300 cārnta tamiḻ nūlkaḷ, pāṭalkaḷ uruppeṟuvataṟku cila pala nūṟu āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉṉarē tamiḻ varivaṭivamum ilakkiyaṅkaḷum tōṉṟat toṭaṅkiyirukka vēṇṭum. koṟkiyil 1970il akaḻnteṭukkappaṭṭa pāṉi ōṭṭil uḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttiṉ kālam ki.mu. 700 eṉa appoḻutē uṟutiyākkap paṭṭatu. koṭumaṇalil aṇmiyil akaḻnta pāṉi ōṭṭu eḻuttuk kaḷiṉ kālamum ki.mu. 500kku muntiyatāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉa irājaṉ (2004) niṟuviyuḷḷār. āticcanallūril 2004il naṭanta akaḻ vāyvil kaṇṭa pāṉi ōṭṭup poṟippiṉ kālamum karimak kaṇippiṉpaṭi ki.mu. 700ai oṭṭiyatāka amiya lām eṉac ceyti vantuḷḷatu. ivaṟṟiyum piṟa ātāraṅ kaḷiyum cuṭṭi irājaṉ (2004), naṭaṉa kācinātaṉ (2004) ākiyōr ''tamiḻukku ki. mu. 800ai oṭṭiyē oru taṉi vari vaṭivam iruntirukkavēṇṭum. ataṉip piṉpaṟṟiyē acōka tamiḻ - pirāmi varivaṭivam (lipi) ki.mu. 300il uruvākkap paṭṭirukkum'' eṉpataṟkāṉa valuvāṉa kāraṇaṅkaḷi muṉ vittuḷḷaṉar. cintuveḷi nākarika lipi tirāviṭa moḻi cārntatu āṉapaṭiyāl pirāmi lipiyum atiliruntē uru vākiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉpar hīrās (New Review; cūli 1936). taṉatu paṇṭit tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ 1938 nūlil ti.nā. cuppiramaṇiyaṉ kūṟiyatiyum niṉivu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
''pirāmi lipi mutalil vaṭamoḻikkāka ēṟ paṭavilliyeṉṟum uyir eḻuttukkaḷuḷ a, i, u ākiya mūṉṟukkum atikac ciṟappiyaḷippatum, meyyeḻuttukkaḷum varkka eḻuttukkaḷik koḷḷātatumāṉa oru pāṣikkeṉa amik kappaṭṭup piṉṉāl vaṭamoḻikku upayōkappaṭum paṭi putiya kuṟikaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum eṇṇa vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. intiyāvil uḷḷa moḻikaḷuḷ ivvitam uḷḷatu tamiḻ oṉṟutāṉ. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ pirāmi mutalil tamiḻukkeṉa aminta lipiyāka iruttal kūṭumā? iḵtu ārāytaṟkuriyatu". 
vaṭanāṭṭu varalāṟṟaṟiñar es. ār. kōyal (1979) karuttum acōka pirāmi eḻuttu paṭippaṭiyāka (vaṭanāṭṭil uruvāṉa talla; atu oru kālakaṭṭattil (ēṟattāḻa ki.mu. 300 eṉak kūṟalām. putitāka uruvākkappaṭṭatu eṉpatākum.
75. tamiḻnāṭṭil irunta ki.mu. 1900 - ki.mu. 300 cārnta peruṅkaṟkāla nākarikattiṉ toṭarcciyē kaḻaka ilakkiya kālam; annākarikak kūṟukaḷiṟ perumpālāṉavi kaḻaka ilakkiyattil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatum rājaṉ pōṉṟavar kaḷuṭiya aṭutta muṭivākum. āka ivvārāyccikaḷ mēlum muṉṉēṟum poḻutu tolkāppiyattiṉ kālam ki.mu. 300kkum cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ muṟpaṭṭatu eṉa aṉivarum ēṟṟuk koḷḷum vāyppuḷḷatu.
76. ittaruṇattil iṉṉoṉṟiyum kūṟa vēṇṭum. ki.mu. 300kku muṟpaṭṭa kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ eviyumē namakkuk kiṭikkavilli . ki.mu. 300 - kipi. 300 kālakaṭṭattic cārnta caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷum muḻumiyāka namakkuk kiṭikkavilli. ki.pi. 300ai oṭṭik kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷit tokuttavarkaḷ evvevaṟṟi viṭṭu vikkalām eṉṟu karutiṉārkaḷō avaṟṟi maṭṭum tokuttu viṭṭu maṟṟavaṟṟi nīkkiviṭṭaṉar. tokuttavaṟṟilum māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceytirukkalām. nuṇṇaṟi vuṭiyavarkaḷ itupaṟṟi 1877-liruntu terivittuḷḷavaṟṟik kāṇpōm : -

cārlas ī kōvar : teṉṉintiya nāṭṭār pāṭal kaḷ (The Folk Songs of Southern India) 1871: tiṭṭa miṭṭuc citikkappaṭāta allatu māṟṟi yeḻutappaṭāta tolpaḻaṅ kālat tamiḻ nūl etiyum iṉṟu kāṇpatu aritu. tamiḻ makkaḷ - tirāviṭa (paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattik kiviṭṭu purāṇakkatikaḷi nampiṉāltāṉ taṅkaḷukkup piḻippu uṇṭu eṉṟu uṇarntavarkaḷē ivvāṟu paḻantamiḻ ilak kiyattic citittavarkaḷ āvar. (taṉit tamiḻc ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷ pala matippiḻantu oḻiyumpaṭi avarkaḷ ceytaṉar. aṟavē oḻikka muṭiyāta nērvukaḷilum annūlkaḷi ayōk kiyat taṉamākac citittu urumāṟṟi ulavaviṭṭu mūla nūl kaḷiṉ karutti uṇara muṭiyātavāṟu ceytuviṭṭaṉar. civattampi (1986yum ikkaruttiṉarē.
hayūnevil : ti tapropēṉiyaṉ (The Taprobaniam) cūṉ 1896 "maturit tamiḻc caṅkamum ataṉ nūlakamum aḻikkap paṭṭa poḻutu paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷ cakaṭṭumēṉikku aḻikkap paṭṭaṉa. (avaṟṟuḷ camaṇak koḷki vāṭi irukkalām eṉṟa karuttil). īḻattil makāvamcam iruntatupōl pāṇṭi yarkaḷ varalāṟṟik kūṟum oru nūl maturic caṅka nūlkaḷil oṉṟāka irunta aḻikkappaṭṭu ataṟkup patilāka tiruviḷi yāṭaṟ purāṇam uruvākkappaṭṭu irukka vēṇṭum aḻikkap paṭṭa varalāṟṟu nūliṉ citivukaḷuṭaṉ, aiṅkamarkaḷ (pārp paṉiya civa matattavarkaḷ ) eṇṇam, camayam ākiyavaṟṟukkup poruttamāṉa puṉi katikaḷuṭaṉ piṉṉip piṇintu ap purāṇam uruvākkappaṭṭatu.
jāṉ spiyars "ñāṉam tōṉṟiya iṭattiliruntu " vālyūs Values II - 10 cūli 1957 : "cintuveḷi nākarika muttirikaḷil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa (tol tamiḻ vācakaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kaṭal koṇṭa (aḻintu pōṉa) paṇṭi nūlkaḷip paṟṟit tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum puttar kālamākiya ki.mu. 600kku muntiya nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟu kūṭa kiṭik kātataṟkāṉa kāraṇatti nām uṇara muṭikiṟatu. (tol tamiḻaruṭiya) paṇṭiya āvaṇaṅkaḷi āriyarkaḷ oṉṟu viṭāmal aḻittuviṭṭaṉar eṉpatē atu.

te.po. mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉ : tolkāppiyam - collatikāram : kallāṭar uri (1971) : ''tol kāppiyatti ataṉ āciriyar enta uruvattil viṭṭuc ceṉ ṟārō anta vaṭivattil atu namakku vantu cēravilli'' (tolkāppiyam : poruḷatikāram marapiyalil pala iṭic cerukalkaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉpatu tolkāppiya aṟiñarkaḷ ti. cavarirāyaṉ, kā.cuppiramaṇiya piḷḷi , cōmacuntara pārati, veḷḷi vāraṇaṉ, ilakkuvaṉār, kamil cuvelapil, irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ, ce.vi. caṇmukam pōṉṟavar ēṟ ṟuḷḷatākum. kuṟippāka nālvaruṇam paṟṟik kūṟum marapiyal cūttiraṅkaḷ 71 - 85 iṭic cerukal eṉpatu ivvaṟiñarkaḷum (ēṉ naṭuniliyāḷar aṉivarum ēṟpatākum. 
tolkāppiyam
tolkāppiyattiṉ kālam ki.mu. 5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari eṉpatip perumpālāṉa naṭunili aṟiñar ēṟkiṉṟaṉar. camskirutattukku (pāṣikku ilakkaṇam varinta pāṇiṉiyiṉ kālam ki.mu. 5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu. pāṇiṉikku muntiya camskiruta ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷum uṇṭu. vaṭa intiyāviṟkuḷ nākarikamaṟṟa āriyar nuḻinta ki.mu 1500kku muṟpaṭṭa kālattil intiyāveṅkum tamiḻiya moḻikaḷ iruntaṉa. ivaṟṟukku lipiyum iruntatu. ilakkiya vaṭivaṅkaḷum iruntu avaṟṟiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷum karuttukkaḷum rik vētattilum ēṟiyuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu uṇmi. eṉavē tol kāppiyamō allatu ataṟku muntiya tamiḻ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷō ("eṉpa". " eṉmaṉār pulavar" pōṉṟavi ivaṟṟic cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa) pāṇiṉikku muṟpaṭṭaviyāka iruntirukkalām eṉac cila tamiḻaṟiñar karutuvatu ātāramaṟṟa kūṟṟu alla. "tolkāppiyar' eḻutiyataṉāl "tolkāppiyam" eṉap peyar peṟṟatu eṉpatu paṇṭu toṭṭu varum marapu. āyiṉum tolkāppiyam valla aṟiñarkaḷ, ilakkuvaṉār, ca. vē. cuppira maṇiyaṉ, poṟkō, culevapil pōṉṟavarkaḷ "tol + kāppu + iyam" - tolkāppiyam, atāvatu toṉ marapik kākkum nūl eṉak kāraṇap peyar peṟṟatu eṉpatum poruttamākavē uḷḷatu. tolkāppiya eḻuttu, col atikāraṅkaḷilum poruḷ atikāra akattiṇi, puṟattiṇi iyalkaḷilum cila māṟṟaṅkaḷ / iṭic cerukalkaḷ piṟ kālattil ceyyappaṭṭirukkalām eṉpatum poruḷatikārattiṉ iṟuti ēḻu iyalkaḷilum māṟṟaṅkaḷ / iṭic cerukal mikappala eṉpatum cuvelapil, mār pōṉṟōr karuttu. pirāmaṇiya ātikkam mikunta kālattil tol kāppiyam muḻumiyāka aḻittoḻikkappaṭāmal pātukāp pataṟkeṉṟē tamiḻ, tamiḻar pāl paṟṟuḷḷavarkaḷum cila iṭiccerukalkaḷi virakākac ceytirukkalām eṉpar pāvāṇar. (eṉiṉum tolkāppiya mutalurikārar iḷam pūraṇar kālamākiya kipi. 17m nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉṉarc ceytu muṭikkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum. tolkāppiyam "māntaṉ aṟivāṟṟal evvaḷavu viyattaku ucca nili eytakkūṭum eṉpatik kāṭṭum ciṟanta cāṉṟukaḷuḷ oṉṟu' One of the finest monuments of human intelligence eṉpar cuvelapil. ilakkiyak koḷkiyil vēṟu entap paṇṭiya moḻi kaḷilum illāta ciṟanta karuttukkaḷit tol poruḷ. ceyyuḷiyal kūṟuvati cuvelapil (1991) kaṭṭuri viḷakku kiṟatu. tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattil kaṭi āṟu iyal kaḷum puṇarcci paṟṟiyaṉa. "tamiḻil puṇarcci paṟṟiya ilakkaṇam taṉittaṉmi koṇṭatu. oṭṭunili viṭṭut taḷarātu, iṉ ṟaḷavum kaṉṉit tamiḻāka" viḷaṅkuvatu, ippuṇarcci paṟṟiya aṟiviyal aṇuku muṟikaḷiṉālēyām" (tamiḻaṇṇal, 2004) 
caṅka kāla akapporuḷ nūlkaḷ
caṅka ilakkiya akapporuḷ pāṭalkaḷil ciṟappi ē.kē. rāmāṉucaṉ (1969) pārāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
toṉmiyāṉaviyē eṉiṉum iṉṟum uyirūṭṭam uḷḷaviyāy ilaṅkum inta (caṅka ilakkiya) akap pāṭal kaḷip pōṉṟa nayam vāyntavi intiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ vēṟu evaṟṟilum illi. appāṭalkaḷ kūṟum vāḻkki neṟiyilum, vivarikkum nikaḻccikaḷilum ciṟanta cemmoḻi ilak kiyattiṉ piṉvarum kūṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kātalōṭu kaṉi vum paṇpāṭum ; veḷippaṭik kūṟṟukkaḷōṭu uḷḷuṟi, iṟicci, aṅkatam ākiyaviyum; talivaṉ, talivi peyar cuṭṭappaṭāviṭiṉum ōviyam pōṉṟa nuṇṇiya varuṇaṉi aṭikaḷ cila avi cuṭṭum poruḷō peritu. tamiḻar aṟivuttiṟaṉiṉ mikat toṉmiyāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭu iv vakappāṭalkaḷ ; atu maṭṭumalla kaṭanta iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭu tamiḻ ilakkiya paṭippil ivaṟṟi viñcuvaṉa illi :
akap pāṭalkaḷiṉ amippu paṟṟi cuvelapil (1986) viḷakkamum karuttiṟ koḷḷat takkatu.
akappāṭal ovvoṉṟum oru kūṟṟu : talivaṉ, talivi, tōḻi, cevili mutaliya cilaruḷ oruvar, nāṭaka māntar pōl, kūṟuvatāka atu amiyum. avarkaḷ oruvaruk koruvarō, taṉakkut tāṉākavō, nilavi nōkkiyō kūṟuvati vācakarkaḷākiya nām kēṭkiṟōm; avvaḷavē. nāṭakak kāṭci oṉṟi muḻumiyāka akappāṭal oṉṟil uḷḷa kūṟṟu vivarittuviṭum. ivi nāṭṭup pāṭalkaḷ pōl virintu pāṭiyavi alla; oru nikaḻcciyik kaṇṭa allatu eṇṇippārtta oru kaviñar atu paṟṟiya tamatu eṇṇaṅkaḷi nērākavō urumāṟṟiyō veḷip paṭuttiya pāṭalkaḷum alla. ivi aracavippāṭalkaḷiṉ naḷiṉam uṭiyavi; "vikalum eṇtēr ceyyum taccaṉ tiṅkaḷ valitta kāl pōl nuṇṇiya pulamittiṟaṉuṭaṉ yākkap paṭṭavi.
mēṟkaṇṭa iruvar kūṟṟukkaḷiṉ āṅkila mūlaṅkaḷ varumāṟu :
“In their antiquity and in their contemporaniety, there is not much else in any Indian literature equal to these quiet and dramatic Tamil poems. In their values and stances they represent a mature classical poetry : passion is balanced by courtesy, transparency by ironies and nuances of design, impersonality by vivid detil, leanness of line by richness of implication. These poems are not just the earliest evidence of the Tamil genius. The Tamils in all their 2000 years of literary effort wrote nothing better". - A.K. Ramanujan.
An Aham poem is always a “Kurru”, an utterance by one of the limited number of dramatis personale, (hero, heroine, heroine's girl-friend, foster mother ete). The reader only overhears what the characters say to each other, to themselves, or to the moon. A poem in this tradition implies, evokes, enacts a drama in a monologue. The poems are not the result of rapid composition like oral epics or as lyrical expessions or sublimations of the poet's own feelings triggered by some romantic event. They are the result of skillful and subtle care, the sophistication of court poetry, "like a chariot wheel made thoughtfully over a month by a carpenter who tossess off eight chariots in a day." K.V. Zvelebil.
puṟanāṉūṟu
puṟanāṉūṟṟil iṉṟu kiṭṭiyuḷḷa 368 pāṭalkaḷiṉ vakippāṭu varumāṟu : cērar - 22; cōḻar - 61; pāṇṭiyar - 31; kuṟunila maṉṉar 134; piṟa - 120. cīṉivācar tam nūlil caṅka kāla varalāṟṟi eḻuta innūṟ ceytikaḷit tiṟampaṭap payaṉ paṭuttiyuḷḷār. (patiṟṟuppattuc ceytikaḷiyum, akap poruḷ paṟṟiya caṅka nūlkaḷil varum ceytikaḷiyum cērttut tāṉ). hārṭ (1999) "āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa teṉ ṉintiyāvi , ēṉ perumaḷavukku āriyarukku muntiya inti yāviyē vivarikkum āvaṇam puṟanāṉūṟu eṉpar" - a testament of pre-Aryan south India and, to a significant extent, of pre Aryan India.'' 
tirukkuṟaḷ
80. tamiḻilakkiyattiṉ taliciṟanta nūl ākiya tiruk kuṟaḷiṉ ciṟappi cīṉivācar pārāṭṭiyuḷḷatik kāṇka. aṟattuppālilum poruṭ pālilum uḷḷa karuttukkaḷ palavaṟṟi camskiruta cāttiraṅkaḷiliruntu vaḷḷuvar eṭuttuk kiyāṇṭuḷḷār eṉṟa cīṉivācar karuttu iṉṟu ēṟkap paṭuvatilli. oru cila orumippāṭukaḷ kuṟaḷukkum vaṭamoḻi aṟanūlkaḷukkum iṭiyē kāṇappaṭumāyiṉum intiyā veṅkum paṇṭu viḷaṅkiya tamiḻiyac cintaṉikaḷiyē iru moḻi nūlkaḷum kiyāṇṭaṉa veṉpatē poruntum.
81. "aṟitōraṟiyāmi kaṇṭaṟṟāl " eṉpatu tiruk kuṟaḷukkum poruntuvatākum. kuṟaḷiṉ ciṟappukkaḷip pārāṭṭip pala moḻi, pala nāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟiyavaṟṟit tokuttāl atuvē oru periya nūlākiviṭum! ulakap periyārkaḷil oruvarāṉa ālperṭ cuviṭcar 1936il veḷiyiṭṭa "intiyac cintaṉiyum ataṉ vaḷarcciyum" (Indian Thought and its Development; Hodder and Stoughton; London; PP. 272) eṉṉum nūliṉ 200 - 205 pakkaṅkaḷil intiyat tat tuvañāṉikaḷil tiruvaḷḷuvar taliciṟantu viḷaṅkuvatip pārāṭṭiyuḷḷār. cila pakutikaḷ varumāṟu:
"intiya makkaḷiṭiyē paṇṭik kālattiliruntē piṟa uyirkaḷ aṉittukkum aṉpu kāṭṭi utavum aṟakkoḷki (the idea of active love) iruntu vantati, intiyap paṇṭi ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇum katikaḷil iruntu aṟikiṟōm. attuṭaṉ kuṟippākat tirukkuṟaḷ eṉṉum nūlil kāṇum aṟavuric ceyyuḷkaḷiliruntum aṟikiṟōm.
"(ulakil piṟa uyirkaḷiṭattu aṉpu kāṭṭic ceyal paṭuvati (life Affirmation) avvaḷavāka valiyuṟuttāta maṉu tarma cāttiram pōṉṟavaṟṟi liruntu māṟupaṭum) kuṟaḷil ulakiyum vāḻkkiyiyum veṟuttu otukkum pōkku (World and life negation) paravalākak kāṇappaṭuvatu alla. eṅkō ōriru iṭattil vēṇṭumāṉāl irukkalām.
''piramaṇīyam, puttamatam, pakavatkīti pōṉṟavaṟṟip pōl immi maṟumip payaṉ karutiyē aṟaneṟiyil niṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟa vāṇikak koḷkiyik kuṟaḷ valiyuṟuttu vatu illi. nallatu ceyvatē takkatu eṉṟa uṇarviṉā lēyē nallatu (aṟam ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟu kuṟaḷ kūṟukiṟatu (kuṟaḷkaḷ 222, 211). pakavatkītiyō karumam ceytu koṇ ṭiruppatu pirapañcaniyati eṉṟu vaṟaṭṭuttaṉamāka valintu kūṟukiṟatu. āṉāl, eṉṉa viyappu, kuṟaḷō maṉitaṉ uḻip patum īṭṭuvatum piṟarukkum piṟa uyirkaḷukkum avaṉ naṉmi ceyvataṟkākattāṉ eṉṟu cāṟṟukiṟatu. (kuṟaḷkaḷ 81,212) pakavat kītippaṭi kaṭami jātikkut takkapaṭi vēṟupaṭum kuṟaḷō makkaḷ aṉivarum nallavi ceytoḻukak" kūṟukiṟatu.''
''ēṉi intiya tarma cāstirakkārar uḻippatil, muyaṟci ceyvatil makiḻvōṭu īṭupāṭu eṉṟu kūṟavilli . kuṟaḷkaḷ 619, 630, pōṉṟaviyē ulakiyalilum, vāḻkkiyilum, ārvattuṭaṉ īṭupaṭumpaṭi aṟivuṟuttu kiṉṟaṉa.
''(peḷattamum kītiyum pōlap paṟṟiṉmi , veṟup piṉmi, kollāmi, akimci ākiyavaṟṟiyum kuṟaḷ valiyuṟuttiṉālum kūṭa atē camayattil) kuṟaḷ atikamāka valiyuṟuttuvatu aṉpum aruḷumēyām (the living ethic of love). kāṇka kuṟaḷkaḷ 72, 78, 79, 103, 226, 241.
''aṟaneṟicār ulaka vāḻkki ilaṭciyattit tiṟam paṭac cuṭṭukiṟatu kuṟaḷ. maṉitaṉiṉ taṉi vāḻkki , piṟa uyir kaḷōṭum ulakattōṭum uṟaḻum avaṉ vāḻkki ākiya vaṟṟil kikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya neṟikaḷik kuṟaḷ viyattaku paṇpuṭaṉum cālpuṭaṉum naṭimuṟikkukanta vakiyil vakuttuḷḷatu. (Characterised by nobility and good sense). ulaka ilakkiyattil vēṟu eṅkum ivvaḷavu ciṟanta oḷimayamāṉa aṟavuri vācakaṅkaḷi vaḻaṅkum nūlik kāṇa iyalātu. (kuṟaḷka ḷ 92, 105, 108, 121,159, 162, 216,298,319,578,594,628,757, 782,874,931,973, 999, 1007, 1024,1032)".
''āka kiṟittuva ūḻit toṭakkak kālattilēyē intiya makkaḷiṭiyē ulaka vāḻkkiyiṭamum uyirkaḷiṭamum paṇpum aṉpum kāṭṭum oru taṉmi iyalpāka iruntu vantuḷḷatik kuṟaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. pirāmaṇiyam, peḷattam, pakavatkīti cār intumatam ākiya vaṟṟiṉ kōṭ pāṭukaḷil illātatu atu. āṉāl kīḻcātiyiṉariṭamum, cātāraṇa makkaḷiṭamum tōṉṟi makkaḷōṭu makkaḷāka vāḻnta ciṟanta camayap pōtakarkaḷ mūlamāka attaṉmi metu metuvāka nāḷaṭivil intu matattil iṭam peṟa lāyiṟṟu. 
cilappatikāramum maṇimēkaliyum
82. cilappatikāramum maṇimēkaliyum cātāraṇa mak kaḷik kāviyat taliviyarākak koṇṭavi; taṉittaṉmi vāyntavi. vañcik kāṇṭatti maṟṟoru pulavar eḻutic cērttirukkalām eṉṟa cīṉivācar karutti mu. aruṇācalamum (1974) ātarikkiṟār. āṉāl cuvalepil (1975) maṟukkiṟār. ivviru kāviyaṅkaḷiṉ kālam ki.pi. 350-450 ākalām eṉpar cuvelapil (1995). kāñcipurattil ki.pi. 5m nūṟṟāṇṭil vacitta tikñāṉar camskirutattil niyāyappiravēca eṉṉum aḷavi nūli eḻutiṉār. annūlukkum maṇimēkaliyiṉ cila pakutikaḷukkum neruṅkiya oppumi iruppatāl maṇi mēkaliyiṉ kālatti kipi 5m nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉ taḷḷu kiṟār viyāpuri. āṉāl maṇimēkaliyi āṅkilattil peyartta ālaṉ tāṉiyalō (Alin Danielou) ikkarutti maṟuttu tamiḻar aḷavi nūṟkaruttukkaḷiyē maṇimēkali āciriyarum, tikñāṉarum payaṉpaṭutti yirukkalām eṉṟum maṇimēkali ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāka irukkalām eṉṟum karutukiṟār.

kaca . kaḷappirar kālam ki.pi 300 - 575)
83. caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kuṟittuḷḷa pāṇṭiya cōḻa cēra maṉṉaruḷ mikap piṟpaṭṭavarkaḷ kālam ki.pi. 3m nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutip pakutiyil muṭivuṟṟatu. piṟkālap pāṇṭiyar āṭci kipi. 560 - 590 mutal āṇṭa kaṭuṅkōṉ kālam mutal toṭaṅkiyatu. piṟkālac cōḻar āṭci vicayālayaṉ (kipi. 848881 mutal toṭaṅkiyatu. (pallava, pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ āḷukiyil cōḻanāṭu kipi 848varit toṭarntatāltāṉ cōḻa parampari ki.pi. 600 ai oṭṭi pāṇṭiyarip pōla mīṇṭum uyir peṟa iyalavilli .
84. kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭavarākiya ciravaṇapeḷakoḷa vaṭ ṭārattu kaḷippu nāṭṭuk kaḷappirar (= kaḷappāḷar) ēṟattāḻa kipi. 250l allatu ataṟkuc caṟṟup piṉṉar tamiḻakattik kippaṟṟi pāṇṭiya cōḻa cēra nāṭukaḷi ki.pi. 575 vari āṇṭaṉar eṉpar ma.cī . veṅkaṭacāmi. avarkaḷ kaṉṉaṭar eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ varumāṟu. 
'kāṉak kaṭicūḻvaṭukak karunāṭar maṉṉaṉ"
- tiruttoṇṭar purāṇam, mūrtti nāyaṉār purāṇam. 
"maturi vavviya karunāṭar vēntaṉ" 		- kallāṭam 
kaḷappirar tamiḻarē eṉṟa cīṉivācar karuttu tavaṟeṉpār vēṅkaṭacāmi. kaḷappirar āṭci tamiḻakattil ki.pi. 450 - 550l maṭṭumē iruntatu eṉṉum muṉṉavar karuttiyum piṉṉavar ēṟṟilar. cōḻa nāṭṭi āṇṭa accuta vikkantaṉ eṉṉum kaḷappira maṉṉaṉip paṟṟi cīṉivācar virivāka eḻutiyuḷḷār.
85. ki.pi. 575ai oṭṭi kaḷappirariṭamiruntu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭip pāṇṭiyaṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ mīṇṭum kippaṟṟiṉāṉ.
'aḷavariya ātirājari akala nīkki akaliṭattik

vēḷvikkuṭi ceppēṭu.

mākkaṭuṅ kōṉ
- paranta vīranārāyaṇaṉ (860-905) taḷavāypuram ceppēṭu. 
atē kipi 575ai oṭṭi kaḷappirariṭamiruntu cōḻanāṭṭi pallava cimmaviṣṇu kippaṟṟik koṇṭāṉ.
piṟkālattil muttariyar eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiya kuṟunila maṉṉarum kaḷappirarum potuvāṉa oru kuṭiyiliruntu pirintu vaḷarntavar eṉpār naṭaṉa kācinātaṉ. (1996)
86. cīṉivācar patiṉeṇ kīḻk kaṇakku nūlkaḷ aṉit tum ki.pi 500 - 800 kālattavi eṉpar. āyiṉum vēṅkaṭacāmi tarum kālavariyaṟi varumāṟu: 
tirukkuṟaḷ, kaḷavaḻi nāṟpatu, - 	ki.pi. 250kku muṟpaṭṭa

potuvākak kaḷappirar āṭciyil camaṇa, putta camayaṅ kaḷ ciṟappaṭintiruntaṉa. eṉavē kipi. 600kkup piṉṉar civa viṇava camayaṅkaḷ taḻitta poḻutu kaḷappirar kālattiya camaṇa, putta camayam cārnta tamiḻkaḷ pala aḻikkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉpar vēṅkaṭacāmi. tiruñāṉa campantar camaṇa ruṭaṉ nikaḻttiya aṉal vāta, puṉalvātac ceytikaḷ itik kuṟippaṉavēyām.
(karu) muṭivuri
87. immuṉṉurikkāṉa ātāra nūṟpaṭṭiyali nūl iṟutiyil kāṇalām. paḻantamiḻar, tamiḻaka varalāṟṟi naṉkaṟiya iṉṟiyamiyāta nūlkaḷil perumpālāṉavi ap paṭṭiyalil uḷḷaṉa. aṇmi āṇṭukaḷil nāṉ eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷ mūṉṟu : vi ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitariṉ tamiḻariṉ tōṟṟamum paravalum (tamiḻākkam), kāntirāv eḻutiya ''tamiḻa nākarikamum cintuveḷi nākarikamum" (tami ḻākka m);Nostratics the light from Tamil according to Devaneyan. ivi mūṉṟum eḻuti veḷivara mutaṟkāraṇar tamiḻmaṇ patippaka iḷavaḻakaṉārē āvār. inta nūliyum eḻutu eḻutu eṉa ūkkuvittu eḻuta vittavarum avarē. avarukku eṉṟum naṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
88. toṭakka kālam mutal ki.pi 600 varit tamiḻakam, intiyā, ulakiṉ piṟa pakutikaḷ cārnta varalāṟṟukku muntiya kāla varalāṟṟuk kāla, nikaḻvukaḷil mutaṉmiyāṉavi kāla varicippaṭi nūl iṟutiyil tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

muṉṉuri
kalvi āṇṭu 1928-29l (ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattil) intiya varalāṟṟu āyvaṟiñar (Reader) āka iruntu nikaḻttiya āyvil uruvākiyatu ipputtakam. ivvāṇṭu cūlittiṅkaḷil innūl nutaliya poruḷiṉ oru pakuti paṟṟi pal kalikkaḻakap pēruri nikaḻtti yuḷḷēṉ. poruṭ kuṟippakarātiyit tokukka utaviya ē. appāturi aiyarukku eṉ naṉṟiyit terivikkiṟēṉ.
āciriyar 
pi.ṭi. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār

aṟimukam
'varalāṟu' eṉpatu etu?
1. vīrattiṉ peyarāl pōrkkaḷattil ērāḷamāṉa māntar paṭukolik kāḷāki ataṉ kāraṇamāka aracaparampari kaḷil oṉṟu oḻintu iṉṉoṉṟu āṭcikku varukiṟatu; kaṭumiyāka uḻittu amitiyāṉa piṟa pakuti makkaḷ īṭṭi vittirukkum celvattik koḷḷiyiṭum ārvattuṭaṉ perum iṉakkuḻukkaḷ (tribes) pulampeyarntu ulakap paṭat tiyē māṟṟukiṉṟaṉa; aracakulak kaṉṉiyar vaṉpuṇarccik kāḷākiṉṟaṉar; akkoṭumikkup paḻikkup paḻiyāka naṭakkum pōril (appāvikaḷ uṭpaṭa) palar irattam cintukiṟatu; ivaṟṟiyellām kūṟuvatutāṉ varalāṟu eṉṟāl kipi 600 vari "varalāṟē " illāta iṉiya nāṭāka tamiḻ intiyā (tamiḻakam) iruntatu eṉalām.
2. māṟāka, puviyiyaṟ cūḻal kāraṇamāka oru makkaḷiṉ camutāya, camayavāḻvu accūḻalukkēṟpa paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarcci yaṭikiṟatu; avvāṟu vaḷarum poḻutu vēṟu vakiyāṉa paṇpāṭṭuṭaṉ vaḷarntuḷḷavarkaḷ tākkamum ēṟpaṭukiṟatu; ammakkaḷ uṇavu, uṭi, poḻutupōkku, kātal vāḻvu, pāṭṭu, kūttu, avarkaḷ aracariyum kaṭavuḷariyum vaḻuttiya vaki, ivi ellām metuvāka māṟukiṉṟaṉa; avarkaḷ uḷ nāṭṭu vaṇikam ceyvatuṭaṉ aṇṭi ayal nāṭukaḷuṭaṉum vaṇikat toṭarpu koḷkiṉṟaṉar; eḷiya muṟiyil uruvākum avarkaḷ ilakkiyap paṭippukaḷ nāḷaṭivil nuṇṇiya, āḻnta ilakkiya marapu koṇṭavi yākac ciṟak kiṉṟaṉa; ivaṟṟiyellām kūṟuvatutāṉ varalāṟu eṉṟāl tamiḻar varalāṟṟi ātimutal ki.pi. 600 vari āyvu ceytu eḻutuvataṟku ērāḷamāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. innūlil ittakiya varalāṟṟi eḻutavē muṟpaṭṭuḷḷēṉ.
3. mānta nākarikattiṉ paḻaṅkaṟkāla, putuk kaṟkālaṅkaḷil palvēṟu nilikaḷ cārnta kaṟkaruvikaḷip pala aṟiñarkaḷ avaravar vāyppuk kēṟpac cēkarittu, avi intiya aruṅkāṭciyakaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil akkālat tamiḻar vāḻviyal paṟṟi eṉatu "intiyāvil kaṟkālam" Stone Age in India (1926) nūlil eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ. tamiḻaruṭiya toṭakkanili irumpuk kāla nākarikak karuvi kaḷiliruntu aṟiyak kūṭiya cila ceytikaḷ, maṟṟum vaṭa intiya āriya nākarikattiṉ tākkam ēṟpaṭumuṉṉarē tamiḻil uruvāki yirunta coṟkaḷ cārnta vērc col āyvāl teriyum pala ceytikaḷ, ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭiyil āriyarukku muntiya tamiḻa nākarikam Pre Aryan Tamil Culture (1929) nūliyum eḻuti yuḷḷēṉ. kālaveḷḷattil aḻiyātu eñci iṉṟu namakkuk kiṭṭiyuḷḷa paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyam, maṟṟum tamiḻar kaḷippaṟṟi camskiruta, pāḷi , kirēkkam, ilattīṉ mutaliya moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, piṟa vaki nūlkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil uḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi āyvu ceytu avaṟṟil kiṭṭiya varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil tamiḻar varalāṟu paṟṟiya innūli eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ. 
ivvaralāṟṟukkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ
4. vēta mantiraṅkaḷiyum pirāmaṇaṅkaḷiyum cērttu vēta ilakkiyam eṉpar. avvēta ilakkiyattilum maṟṟum āriyarkaḷiṉ toṭakka kāla taṉinapar , maṟṟum camūka vāḻkki vitikaḷik kūṟum cūttiraṅkaḷ, itikācaṅkaḷ (irāmāyaṇam, makāpāratam) purāṇaṅkaḷ, toṭakkakkāla pāḷi (putta, artta mākati (camaṇa) ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ākiya aṉittilum uḷḷa tamiḻar varalāṟu (āriyarkaḷukkum tamiḻarukkum iṭi yilāṉa toṭarpu cārnta kuṟippukaḷi nuṇukip pārttu aṟinta ceytikaḷ innūlil payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟi atuvum purāṇac ceytikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttum poḻutu parjitaru (Pargiter) ṭiya āyvuk kaṇṇōṭṭattiyum avar muṭivukaḷiyum nāṉ ēṟattāḻa muḻumi yākap piṉpaṟṟiyuḷḷēṉ. akastiyarkaḷ, vaciṣṭarkaḷ, visvā mittirarkaḷ ākiyōr ikṣvāku maṉṉaṉ kālattiliruntu srī kiruṣṇaṉ kālam vari ettaṉiyō nikaḻvukaḷil vantu vantu pōkiṉṟaṉarē, itu eṉṉa kuḻappam eṉap palarum viyan tuḷḷōm. itaṟku parjitar (Ancient Historical Traditions 1922) aḷittuḷḷa viḷakkam - atāvatu ivi mūṉṟum param pariyāṉa kuṭumpap peyarkaḷ, taṉinapar peyar alla eṉpatu - teḷivu tantuḷḷatu. vaṭa intiya aracap parampariyiṉar paṭṭiyali parjitar ēṟattāḻa poruttamāka nirṇayit tuḷḷār. atiyē nāṉ perumaḷavukkup piṉpaṟṟi, vēta kālattil āriyac caṭaṅkum paṇpāṭum paṭippaṭiyākat teṉ ṉintiyāvil paraviya kāla varampiyum muṟiyiyum nirṇa yittuḷḷēṉ.
5. ayal nāṭṭuc cāṉṟukaḷākiya mecopatāmiyā , ekiptu poṟippukaḷ, kalveṭṭukkaḷum kirēkka, lattīṉ nūlkaḷum mēli nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ intiyāviṉ paṇṭiya vaṇikat toṭarpukaḷip paṟṟi maṭṭumē viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa. "ceṅkaṭaṟ celaviṉ" (Periplus of the Erythren sea) āṅkilap peyarppi veḷiyiṭṭa skāp (Scoff) nūliyum, vār miṅṭaṉ eḻutiya urōmappēraracu - intiya vaṇikam (Commerce between the Roman Empire and India) nūliyum nāṉ āḻntu paṭittup palvēṟu kāla kaṭṭaṅkaḷ cārntu an nūlkaḷ tarum ceytikaḷi intiyac ceytikaḷōṭu iṇittup payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷēṉ.
6. āyiṉum innūlukku eṉatu taliyāṉa ātāram kipi 600kku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻ ilakkiya nūlkaḷi āyvu ceytu nāṉ tokuttuḷḷavi pal tuṟic ceytikaḷēyām. avaṟṟuḷ mikat toṉmi vāyntatu tolkāppiyap poruḷatikāram ākum. tolkāppiya kālattukku (ki.pi allatu 2m nū) muṟpaṭṭa cila nūṟṟāṇṭu cārnta nūlkaḷil kaṇṭa ilakkiya marapukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyilēyē poruḷati kāram eḻutap paṭṭuḷḷatu eṉiṉum, ammarapukaḷ kāṭṭum tamiḻar vāḻviya lāṉatu tolkāppiyattiṟku orāyiram īrāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar vāḻnta tamiḻariṭiyē naṭi muṟiyil irunta paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum paṇpāṭṭiyum kāṭṭuvatāka nāṉ koṇṭuḷḷēṉ. apporuḷatikāratti viṭa mēlum nēraṭiyāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ (kipi. 1-6 nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ cārntavi yāṉa) toki nūlkaḷil (eṭṭuttoki, pattup pāṭṭu uḷḷaṉa. paḻaṅkālat tamiḻilakkiyak kaṭaliṉ oru ciṟu viḻukkāṭē, atuvum attoki nūlkaḷil cērkkap paṭṭamiyāl iṉṟu namakkuk kiṭṭiyuḷḷaṉa. ēṟkeṉavē aṟiñar iruvar ittoki nūlkaḷip payaṉpaṭutti yuḷḷaṉar. mutalāmavar "1800 āṇṭukku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻar" eṉṉum nūli 1904 il eḻutiya vē. kaṉakacapippiḷḷi . paḻantamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷip paṟṟiya pala ceytikaḷum makkaḷ vāḻviyalip paṟṟiya cila ceytikaḷum atil uḷḷaṉa. avar kālattil toki nūṟkaḷil cila accil varavilli yākiyāl ciṟanta tamiḻaṟiñarāka avar irunta pōtilum avar nūlil pala piḻikaḷ nuḻintu ataṉ kāraṇamāka ātāramaṟṟa ceytikaḷ cilavum varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷāka ulavum nili ēṟ paṭṭuḷḷatu. ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka "intiya varalāṟu - tolliyal" pērāciriyar mūtaṟiñar es. kiruṣṇacāmi aiyaṅkār paḻantamiḻilakkiyattil uḷḷa aracarkaḷ, talivarkaḷ cārnta kuṟippukkaḷiyellām tiraṭṭit tam nūlkaḷ - palavaṟṟil viḷakkiyuḷḷār; āyiṉum paṇṭik kālat tamiḻar vāḻ viyalip paṟṟiyō, avvāḻviyaliṉ paṭi muṟi vaḷarc ciyip paṟṟiyō avar eḻutavilli - avvilakkiyattil itu paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ ērāḷamāka irunta pōtilum. paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyattil kāṇum tamiḻar vāḻviyal paṟṟiya ceyti vaḷattil nāṉ innūlil payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷatu nāṉkil oru paṅku kūṭa irukkātu. nāṉ veḷik koṇarntuḷḷa ceytikaḷuḷ perumpālāṉavi itu vari varalāṟṟaṟiñar pārvikku varātaviyākaliṉ perumpālāṉa iyalkaḷil ovvoṉṟilum mūṉṟil oru paṅku paḻantamiḻp pāṭalkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ āṅkila ākkamumākavē uḷḷatu. ivvaḷavu viri vāka ātāraṅkaḷi muntiya varalāṟṟaṟiñar tantilar. tamiḻ teriyātavarkaḷukku atu mikiyākat tōṉṟiṉum vara lāṟṟiyal vaḷarcci karuti immuṟi piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
7. nāṉ āṅkilattil tantuḷḷa paḻantamiḻp pāṭalkaḷil vaḻakkiṟantaviyāṉa pala coṟkaḷum, ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟukkup poruḷ kūṟuvatil iṭikkāla uriyāciriyarkaḷē ciṟcila viṭaṅkaḷil tavaṟu ceytuḷḷaṉar ākaliṉ attakiya iṭaṅkaḷil avarkaḷiṭamiruntu tuṇintu māṟupaṭṭuḷḷēṉ. moḻiyamippilum ilakkaṇa amip pilum tamiḻum āṅkilamum muṟṟilum vēṟāṉavi. mēlum ippāṭalkaḷ aṭi mēl aṭi koṇṭavi; coṟ ṟoṭarkaḷ cikkalāṉa muṟiyil piṉṉip piṇintuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē ceyyuḷ aṭikaḷik kiṭantavāṟē āṅkila ākkam ceyvatu mikak kaṭiṉam. varavāṟṟup payaṉ karutiyē inta āṅkila ākkam eṉpatāl kūṭumāṉavari nēr poruḷ (literal translation) koḷḷavē muyaṉṟuḷḷēṉ – āṅkilattiṟ kuriya marapuc col varampi cila iṭaṅkaḷil mīṟa nēriṭṭa pōtilum. eṉiṉum eṉatu āṅkila ākkattiṉ poruḷ eḷitāka viḷaṅ kum eṉa nampukiṟēṉ. eṉatu moḻipeyarppil tavaṟukaḷ iruntāl tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ eṉakkut terivippārkaḷāka. eṉiṉum mūlappāṭalkaḷiṉ karutti yāṇṭum nāṉ tavaṟākak kūṟiyuḷḷatākak karutavilli . 
kālak kaṇippu
8. ''varalāṟṟukkuk kaṇ kālak kaṇippu eṉṟa kūṟṟiṉpaṭi pārttāl paṇṭi intiya varalāṟu eṉṟum kuruṭākavē irukka vēṇṭiyatutāṉ. eṉiṉum oru makkaḷiṉ vāḻviyaliṉ pariṇāma vaḷarccip paṭimuṟikaḷi viḷakkum poḻutu kuṟippāka iṉṉiṉṉa āṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟa iyalātu; tēviyu milli. paṇṭi intiya varalāṟṟu nikaḻccikaḷiṉ kāla varampukaḷ iṉṟuḷḷatu pōla varuṅkālattilum teḷivāṉa variyaṟi illāmal tāṉ irukkap pōkiṉṟaṉa. "tēcapakti '' cārnta ātāramaṟṟa pukaḻurikaḷukkum, ciṟumi maṉam cārnta ikaḻurikaḷukkum atu iṭam tantu tāṉ varum. vētakālat toṭakkam ki.mu. 1200 eṉṟu mēli nāṭṭu aṟiñar ep poḻutu karutiṉar? ivvulakattiyē karttar ki.mu. 4004l tāṉ paṭittār eṉṟa (ārc piṣap uṣar) kōṭpāṭu nilaviya poḻutu! ippoḻutō aṟiviyalāḷar mānta iṉat tōṟṟam kuṟinta aḷavu oru laṭcam āṇṭukku muṉṉar irukka vēṇṭum eṉa niṟuviyuḷḷaṉar. aṇmiyil arappā, mokeñcotarō akaḻvāyvu veḷik koṇarn tuḷḷa cintu veḷi nākarika eccaṅkaḷ ki.mu. 3000ttukku muntiyavi; avaṟṟilēyē āriyak karuttukkaḷiṉ tākkam pulappaṭāmal illi. āṉālum iṉṟum airōppiya aṟiñarkaḷ, kaṭanta nūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ uruvākki iṉṟum kaṇ mūṭit taṉamākap piṉpaṟṟi varum muṭivāṉa "vētakālat toṭakkam ki.mu. 1200" eṉpataṉi maṟu āyvu ceyya avarkaḷ iciva tilli. parjitar tarum paṇṭik kāla intiya aracar paṭṭiya liṉ paṭi makāpāratap pōrukku muṉṉar aracarkaḷ 90 tali muṟi āṇṭuḷḷaṉar. appōr ki.mu. 15m nūṟṟāṇṭil naṭantatākak karuta ātāram uḷḷatu. āka vētakālat toṭak kam kaṇṭippāka ki.mu. 3000kku muṉṉar irukka vēṇṭum. vēta kāla mut tī vaḻipāṭu uruvākumuṉṉar irunta oṟṟit tī vaḻipāṭu neṭuṅkālam iruntirukka vēṇṭum. āka āriyar tī vaḻipāṭu ki.mu. 3000lāvatu uruvākiyirukka vēṇṭum. itu paṟṟi virivāka āyvu ceyya iṅku iyalātu. eṉiṉum nāṉ piṉvarum aṭippaṭiyi mēṟ koṇṭuḷḷēṉ. atāvatu vēta kālam ēṟattāḻa ki.mu. 3000-1500 eṉak koṇṭuḷḷēṉ. ovvoṉṟum 500 āṇṭu koṇṭa mūṉṟu yukaṅkaḷāka anta āyirattinnūṟu āṇṭukaḷiyum pirikkalām. iraṇṭāvatu yukak kaṭiciyil atāvatu ki.mu. 2000l rāmaccantiraṉ - vāḻntāṉ. avaṉ piṟanta poḻutu aintu uṭukkaḷ ēṟu mukattil iruntaṉa eṉṟa paramparic ceytiyum iti uṟuti ceykiṟatu. srī rāmaṉ kālattukkum srī kiruṣṇaṉ kālat tukkum iṭiyē 500 āṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟirukka vēṇṭum. kiruṣṇaṉ cāvuṭaṉ kaliyukam piṟakkiṟatu. (vēta mantira riṣi kaḷiṉ paramparikaḷum attuṭaṉ muṭivuṟṟu vēta kālamum muṭikiṟatu eṉalām. pāratap pōrukkup piṉṉar intiya varalāṟṟil kālak kaṇippu caṟṟu eḷitākiṟatu, eṉiṉum aṉivarum ēṟṟuk koḷḷak kūṭiya tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa kālak kaṇippu ippoḻutum illi. eṉiṉum nikaḻccikaḷiṉ kāla variciyē namakkut tēviyākaliṉ uṟutiyāṉa āṇṭu varampu tēviyilli (nirṇayippatum iyalvatalla).
9. teṉ intiyāvip porutta variyil mēṟkaṇṭa mēmpōkkāṉa teḷivaṟṟa kālak kaṇippu varampukaḷik kūṭa nirṇayikka iyalavilli. cila aracarkaḷiṉ kāla varampi nampakamāka nirṇayittu viṭalām eṉa kaṉakacapi niṉittār; āṉāl atu māyi ākiviṭṭatu. ki.pi. 600 vari nikaḻcci kaḷiṉ kāla variciyip paṟṟi maṭṭumē nām aṟiya iyalkiṟatu. ataṟkup pintiya kālattukku cila kalveṭṭukkaḷ aṭip paṭiyil kālak kaṇippukkaḷi ōraḷavukkuc ceyya iyal kiṟatu.

iyal 1
tamiḻariṉ paṇṭiya nākarikam,
paṇpāṭṭiṟkāṉa puviyiyal aṭippaṭi
tamiḻar teṉ intiyāviṉ tolkuṭikaḷ
1. oru makkaḷ vāḻum nilappakutikku iyintatāka avar kaḷ paṇpāṭu amintirukkiṟatu; avarkaḷ piṟa makkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koḷḷumuṉṉarē anta nilap pakutiyilēyē appaṇpāṭu uruvāṉataṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷum uḷḷaṉa; ip paṭiyiruntāl eṉṉa poruḷ? annilap pakutiyiṉ puviyiyal aṭippaṭitāṉ avarkaḷ paṇpāṭṭi uruvākkiyuḷḷatu eṉpatē poruḷ. iyaṟkic cūḻnilikkēṟpa uruvākiya paṇpāṭṭukku aṭippaṭik kāraṇam puviyiyal tāṉ; paṭi yeṭuppu, vaṇikam, pōṉṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷāl ap paṇpāṭṭuṭaṉ toṭarpu koṇṭa piṟa makkaḷ cārnta varalāṟṟuk kāraṇam alla. tamiḻakattil paṇṭik kālattiliruntu māntaṉ paṇpāṭum nākarikamum, puviyiyaṟ cūḻnilik kēṟpa paṭippaṭiyāka uruvāṉati nām niṟuva iyalkiṟatu. intiya maṇ māḻi vaḷam koṇṭatu; aḷaviṟanta uyiriṉa, nilit tiṇi vaḷamuṭiyatu; ataṉ taṭpa veppanili cila pakutikaḷil tīvukaḷil uḷḷatu pōlum, cila pakutikaḷil peruṅ kaṇṭa uṭpakutikaḷil uḷḷatu pōlum, iruppatu mānta iṉam pala tuṟiyilum vaḷarcciyaṭiyattakkatāka uḷḷatu. ivvaḷavum iruntum intiya varalāṟṟāciriyar palar intiyāvukku appāl uḷḷa vaḷam kuṟinta nāṭukaḷiliruntu tāṉ makkaḷ iṅku vantu kuṭiyēṟiyirukka vēṇṭum; avvāṟu vantēṟikaḷ tāṉ intiyāvil palvēṟu paṇpāṭu, nākarikaṅ kaḷi uruvākkiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatit tamatu kōṭ pāṭākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉar! paṇṭit "tirāviṭarkaḷ'' vaṭa mēṟku intiya, vaṭakiḻakku intiya, malik kaṇavāykaḷ vaḻiyāka ēṟkeṉavē ayal nāṭukaḷil uruvākiyirunta paṇ pāṭṭuṭaṉ intiyāviṟkuḷ pukuntu teṟkē kāviri, vikip pakuti vari vantu kuṭiyēṟiyatāka ivvaralāṟṟāciri yar ceppukiṉṟaṉar. (payaṇa vacatiyuḷḷa nam kālattil cuṟṟulā vaḻi kāṭṭi nūl oṉṟu, nāṭu viṭṭu nāṭu payaṇam ceyya eḷiya vaḻi kāṭṭuvatu pōla, ivarkaḷ 'tirāviṭar'' kaḷic cuḷuvāka eṅkiruntō intiyāvukku vaṭa mēṟku, vaṭa kiḻakkiliruntu vanta vantēṟikaḷākat tamiḻakattiṟkuḷ kuṭiyēṟṟukiṉṟaṉar!) vaṭamēṟku intiyāvil (palucis tāṉattil pēcappaṭum pirāhay moḻiyil tamiḻōṭu iyipuṭiya cila coṟkaḷ iruppatiyē ātāramākak koḷkiṟārkaḷ ivarkaḷ. anta iyipukku uṇmiyāṉa kāraṇam paṇṭik kālattil intiyā muḻuvatum (vaṭa mēṟkup pakuti uṭpaṭa) tamiḻō atiyotta vēṟu moḻiyō vaḻaṅki vantatu tāṉ. iṉṟu camskirutak kuṭumpa moḻikaḷākak karutappaṭum inti mutaliya vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭi ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷiyum collamippiyum koṇṭavi tām ; vaṭa intiya moḻi vākkiyam oṉṟip poruḷ citiyāmal tirāviṭa moḻikku māṟṟa vēṇṭumāṉāl coṟkaḷi maṭṭum māṟṟiṉāl pōtum. tamiḻiya moḻikaḷ oru kālattil intiyā muḻuvatum paraviyiruntaṉa eṉpatit tāṉ itu niṟuvukiṟatu; intiyāvukkuḷ tirāviṭarkaḷ vantēṟi kaḷāka vaṭakkiliruntu nuḻintavarkaḷ eṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭi itu poy yākkukiṟatu. intiyā muḻuvatum paṇṭu paravi vāḻntu vanta (tamiḻiya) makkaḷi intiyāviṉ paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ alla eṉṟu koḷvataṟkāṉa enta ātāramum illi.
2. paḻaṅkaṟkālattiliruntu māḻik kālam variyuḷḷa nākarikaṅkaḷ cārnta teṉṉintiya tolliyal kaṇṭupiṭippu kaḷum iṅku nākarikam, paṇpāṭṭu vaḷarcci iṭiviṭātu paṭippaṭiyāka nikaḻntuḷḷatu eṉṟum (ayal nākarika nuḻivu kāraṇamāka ēṟpaṭṭa iṭiyīṭu illi eṉṟum kāṭṭu kiṉṟaṉa. inta neṭuṅkālam muḻuvatumē tamiḻ moḻiyum teṉṉintiyāvil iruntu vantuḷḷatu. paṭippaṭiyāṉa nākarika vaḷarcciyil ovvoru paṭi muṟi vaḷarcciyiyum kāṭṭat tēviyāṉa coṟkaḷ paḻiya (caṅka ilakkiyattil uḷḷaṉa. appaṇṭikkāla paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷi (pācil vaṭivaṅkaḷ pōla) attamiḻilakkiyattil iṉṟum kāṇalām. āka tamiḻarkaḷ teṉṉintiyāviṉ paḻaṅkuṭikaḷ eṉa uṟutiyākak kūṟalām.

aintiṇikaḷ
3. puviyiṉ mēṟparappil maṉitar vāḻak kūṭiya pakutikaḷi aintu vakiyākap pakukkalām eṉak kaṇṭa paṇṭit tamiḻar avvāṟē pakuttu ovvoṉṟiyum oru tiṇi eṉṟaṉar. tiṇ/ tiṭ= nilappakuti - tiṇi. avarkaḷ pakutta aintu tiṇikaḷ : kuṟiñci, malip pakuti ; pāli (nīraṟṟa pāli); mulli (malippakutikkum camaveḷippakutikkum iṭip paṭṭa kāṉakap pakuti); marutam (āṟukaḷ pāyum camaveḷi); neytal (kaṭaṟkari) ākiyaviyām. tiṇi eṉṉum col cila viṭaṅkaḷil puvi eṉṟa poruḷilum vaḻaṅkukiṟatu. ovvoru tiṇiyilum uḷḷa cūḻalukku ēṟpa aṅku vāḻum maṉitar vāḻviyalum amikiṟatu eṉpatip paṇṭiyat tamiḻar kaṇṭaṉar. tamiḻakattil ciṟiya aḷavil aintu tiṇikaḷum aṭuttaṭuttuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭit teṉṉintiyaṉ oru tiṇiyi liruntu maṟṟa tiṇikkup paraviya poḻutu antantat tiṇik kēṟṟavāṟu avaṉ vāḻviyal māṟiyatu.
4. mānta iṉa nākarika vaḷarcciyil naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkari nāṭukaḷ, ālps malippakuti, nārtik (airōppiya - āciyak kaṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaṭa pakuti) ākiya mūṉṟu pakutikaḷil ov voṉṟilum mānta iṉa vāḻviyal vēṟupaṭṭuḷḷati māntaviyalāḷar kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar. naṇṇilak kaṭal cūḻ pakuti nākarikaṅkaḷi āyvu ceytu mutaṟ pakutiyip paṟṟi aṟintaṉar. piṉṉar avvāṟē piṉṉiraṇṭu pakutikaḷip paṟṟiyum āyvu ceytu avaṟṟip paṟṟi aṟintaṉar. tamiḻakattiṇip pakuppiṉ aṭippaṭiyil ivi mūṉṟiyum muṟiyē neytal, kuṟiñci, mulli eṉalām. (paṇṭik kālattil airōppāvilum āṟṟuc camaveḷikaḷil marutatti yotta tiṇi vāḻviyal iruntatu. āyiṉum 19vatu nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē akkaṇ ṭam muṟṟilum toḻil mayamāka ākiviṭṭatāl paṇṭiya airōppiya maruta nilap paṇpāṭu iṉṟu nēraṭiyākat terintu koḷḷa muṭiyāta paṭi oḷintuḷḷatu. āppirikkāvilum āciyāvilum uḷḷatu pōla airōppiyāvil pāli vaṉam illi. pālivaṉa nāṭōṭikaḷākiya perīyiṉkaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷ vāḻviyalip paṟṟiyum māntaviyal āyvukaḷ kuṟivē. āka mānta iṉa nākarikamum paṇpāṭum aintu nilaṅkaḷit tāṇṭi vaḷarntuḷḷatu : kuṟiñci - vēṭar , pāli - nāṭōṭi, mulli - āyar, neytal - kaṭal cār vāḻviyal; marutam - vēḷāṇmi (aṇmik kālat toḻil vaḷarcci uṭpaṭa). antantat tiṇic cūḻalukku ēṟpa appakutiyiṉ nākarika vaḷarcci amintatu. 
vēṭar
5. teṉ intiyāvil maṉitaṉ mutalil kuṭiyēṟiya pakuti kuṟiñci takkāṇa pīṭapūmi. āṇṭu tōṟum paruvakkāṟṟup perum maḻiyāl karintu tēyntu uruvāṉa (atika uyara millāta) malikaḷik koṇṭa pakuti atu. appakutikku caṟṟut tāḻvāka taṇṭakāraṇiyam (aracaṉ taṇṭakaṉ peyar koṇṭatu) eṉṉum aṭarnta veppanilakkāṭukaḷ koṇṭa pakuti iruntatu. akkāṭṭil maṉitaṉukkup pōṭṭi yāka ciṅkam, puli, yāṉi, kāṭṭerumi malippāmpu pōṉṟaviyum avaṟṟi viṭa maṉitaṉik kaṭumiyākap pātitta ciṟṟuyirkaḷum (malēriyāk kocu pōṉṟavi) iruntaṉa. vilaṅkukaḷiṭamiruntu taṉṉik kāttuk koḷḷa kuṟiñcip pakutiyil uḷḷa iyaṟkiyāṉa kukikaḷiyum perum pāṟikaḷiyum āti maṉitaṉ nāṭiṉāṉ. appoḻutu nīr cērttu vikka maṇ pāṉi kiṭiyātu; malippakutiyil uḷḷa kuḻikaḷ, paḷḷaṅkaḷil uḷḷa nīrip parukiṉāṉ. kālukkaṭiyil kiṭanta kaṟkaḷē avaṉuṭiya mutal paṭikkalaṉ ākum. palvēṟu vakik kaṟkaḷi (kaṟkaḷiyē karuviyākak koṇṭu uṭittum citittum kūrākkiyum kōṭāli muṉi, vēl muṉi, vāḷmuṉi, curaṇṭi ākiyavaṟṟi uruvākkak kaṟṟuk koṇṭāṉ. kuṟiñciyil ivvāṟu ''paḻaṅkaṟkāla nākarikam" uruvāyiṟṟu. ikkālam cārnta kal āyutaṅkaḷ kaṭappi, nellūr, vaṭārkkāṭu, ceṅkalpaṭṭu māvaṭṭak kuṟiñcip pakutiyil kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.
6. kuṟiñcinila māntaṉ mutalil paḻam, koṭṭi , kiḻaṅku ākiyavaṟṟi uṇṭāṉ. ivi cila kālaṅkaḷil muḻumiyākak kiṭikkāmiyiṉ vilaṅkukaḷiṉ ūṉiyum uṇṇat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. piṟa vilaṅkukaḷiṭamiruntu kāttuk koḷvati viṭa, uṇavukku ūṉ peṟuvataṟkākavē māntaṉ mutalil ciṟanta vēṭṭikkāraṉ āṉāṉ. maṉitaṉiṉ mutal toḻil vēṭṭi yāṭuvatu. ulakeṅkilum uḷḷa paḻaṅkaṟkālak karuvikaḷ oṉṟu pōl uḷḷaṉa. toṭakka kāla vēṭṭikkāraṉ niliyilēyē māntaṉ ulakeṅkum paraviviṭṭāṉ eṉpati itu kāṭṭukiṟatu.
7. kuṟiñciyiṉ cūḻal kāraṇamākavē vēṭṭit toḻiṟ kāla māntaṉuṭiya iru perum kaṇṭupiṭippukaḷ nikaḻntaṉa: oṉṟu vil - ampu; maṟṟatu tīyuṇṭākkum muṟi. teṉṉintiyak kāṉakap pakutiyil ērāḷamāka vaḷarvatu mūṅkil kuṟiñci makkaḷākiya kuṟavar mūṅkilum ataṉ pātikaḷum vaḷintu nimirum taṉmi koṇṭiruttalik kaṇṭu avaṟṟi vaḷittu, kāṭṭuk koṭikaḷāl muṉikaḷik kaṭṭi, vil uruvākki atiliruntu īrāṉa neṭumuḷ, kuccikaḷi ampāka viṭukkak kaṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar. ippaṭi uruvāṉa vil - ampup paṭikkalaṉil intiya vīrarē viṟpaṉṉarkaḷ : pāracīka aracarkaḷ ṭēriyas, corkcas pōrp paṭikaḷiṉ oru pirivāka intiyā vil vīrar paṭiyum perumiyuṭaṉ paṅkēṟṟatu. iṉṟum orē ampil puliyik kollum āṟṟal peṟṟa intiya vēṭṭikkārar uḷḷaṉar.
8. toṭakka kālak kuṟavaruṭiya maṟṟiya kaṇṭupiṭippu tī yuṇṭākkum muṟi. kuṟiñcip pakutikaḷil kaṭuṅkāṟṟil oṉṟōṭoṉṟu uraci mūṅkil tī piṭippatik kaṇṭa kuṟiñci makkaḷ iru kaṭṭikaḷi uracit tī uṇṭākkak kaṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar. vēṭṭiyāṭiya vilaṅkiṉ ūṉic cuṭṭut tiṉṉa mutalil tī payaṉpaṭṭatu. 
kuṟiñci nilap peṇkaḷ
9. kuṟiñci nila āṇkaḷ vēṭṭiyāṭac ceṉṟa nili yil peṇkaḷ paḻam poṟukkiṉar; kiḻaṅku tōṇṭiṉar; taṅkaḷ kuṭiyiruppic cuṟṟit tāmākavē muḷitta kāṭṭarici, mūṅkilarici, tiṇi ākiya kūlaṅkaḷic cēkarittaṉar.
10. peṇkaḷiṉ maṟṟiya vēli kuḻantikaḷip pārttuk koḷvatu. ikkaṭṭattil māntar vīṭu kaṭṭak kaṟṟilar; ataṟkut tēviyum illi; teṉṉintiyat taṭpa veppaniliyil maraṅkaḷ, pāṟikaḷ, kukikaḷ tarum maṟippē veyil maḻikkup pōtumāṉatāka iruntatu (ātiyil māntaṉ vīṭu kaṭṭiyatu taṅkuvataṟkāka maṭṭumalla; ati viṭa mukkiyamāka tāṉ cēkaritta uṇavup poruḷkaḷākiya uṭimic celvattip pātukāttu vikkavē; paḻaṅ kaṟkālat toṭakkattil uṇavup poruḷkaḷic cērttu vikkum tēvi āti maṉitaṉukku ēṟpaṭavilli). niliyāṉa kuṭiyiruppu illi; aṭikkaṭi iṭamāṟṟam; eṉavē āṭa variṭiyē kuṭumpa uṇarvu mutalil tōṉṟavilli. iṉak kuḻu māntariṭiyē tāyvaḻik kuṭumpa muṟi (Matriarchal family) uruvāṉataṟku ituvē kāraṇam.
11. tāyvaḻik kuṭumpa muṟikku maṟṟoru kāraṇam . tirumaṇac caṭaṅkukaḷum, tirumaṇa muṟik kaṭṭuppāṭum āti māntariṭam kaṭumi yāṉaviyāka illi eṉpatām. kaṇṭatum kātal ; uṭaṉaṭip puṇarcci, tirumaṇattiṟku muṉṉarō piṉṉarō kuḻuviṉarukku viruntu ; ivaṟṟukku mēl virivāṉa caṭaṅkukaḷ illi. tirumaṇa iṇippum niliyāka nīṭit tirukkātu eṉak karutalām. taṉi napar cottu iṉmi , niliyāṉa kuṭiyiruppu iṉmi, ivaṟṟōṭu tirumaṇam paṟṟiya inta nilimiyum tāyvaḻic camutāyam neṭunāḷ iruntataṟku oru kāraṇamākum.
12. māntarukku, atuvum makaḷirukkut tammi aḻaku paṭuttuvatil taṇiyāta ārvam uṇṭu. kuṟavar iṉap peṇkaḷ ōyvu nērattil cōḻikaḷ, maṇikaḷik kōttu aṇintu koṇṭaṉar. āṭavar tām koṉṟa puliyiṉ pallik koṇarntu koṭukka ataṉiyum māliyil kōttuk koṇṭaṉar. piṟkālattil teṉṉintiya makaḷir tirumaṇa māṉataṟku aṭiyāḷamāka (kaṇavaṉ irukkum vari) aṇiyum tālikku muṉṉōṭi itu. makaḷir iṭuppil aṇinta taḻi yuṭi iṉṉoru alaṅkāram. teṉṉintiyap paḻaṅkuṭi kaḷil mikavum piṟpaṭṭa niliyil uḷḷavarkaḷ iṉṟum taḻiyuṭi aṇikiṉṟaṉar. 
pāli nila makkaḷ
13. pālip pakutiyāṉa vemmiyāṉa maṇaṟ pakuti puviyil makkaḷ niliyāka vāḻum iṭamākap perumaḷavukku illi. kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭit turattic cellum vēṭaṉ cila vēḷikaḷil pālip pakutiyil cila nāḷ taṅka nērum. āyiṉum uṭal valuvum neñcuramum, uḷḷa āṭavarum nāṭu kāṇum āvalil pālip pakutikkuc ceṉṟu taṅkiyirukkalām. pālip pakutikkuc ceṉṟu cila nāḷō niliyākavō vāḻnta makkaḷ maṟavarum kaḷvarum (maṟam - maṟavar ; kaḷ = valimi - kaḷiṟu - āṇ yāṉi / cuṟāmīṉ ; kaḷ = (valimi tarum ) matu ; kaḷam - pōrkkaḷam ) . pāli vaḷa maṟṟatākaliṉ annila makkaḷākiya paṭippayiṟci mikka maṟavarum kaḷḷarum piṟkālattil pōrvīrarkaḷāyiṉar; piṟa tiṇiyil vāḻnta pōrkkuṇam kuṟinta celvarkaḷic cūṟiyāṭiṉar; eṉavē "maṟam" koṭumiyiyum ''kaḷvar" tiruṭariyum kuṟikkalāyiṟṟu. āṉāl āti kālattil putu nilam kāṇutalilum vīram kāṭṭutalilum ārvamikka āṭavarkaḷē pāliyiṟ kuṭiyēṟiṉar. pāli nila āṭavar maṟac ceyalkaḷilum pōril lum īṭupaṭṭirukkum kālaṅkaḷil, peṇkaḷum kuḻanti kaḷumē taṉittirupparātaliṉ pāli nila makkaḷiṭamum tāy vaḻic camuka muṟiyē taḻittatu.
āyar
14. kuṟiñciyil makkaḷ toki perukiyatu; uṇavukkut taṭṭuppāṭu ēṟpaṭṭatu; māntar aṭuttuḷḷa kāṉakap pakutiyāṉa mullikkuc ceṉṟaṉar. itaṟkuḷḷāka iṉṉoru nākarika vaḷarcciyiyum māntaṉ aṭin tiruntāṉ. atu tāṉ vilaṅkukaḷip paḻakki vaḷarttal vēṭṭikkup pērutaviyāṉa nāy, kuṟavar niliyilēyē paḻakkappaṭṭirukkum. ēṉiya erumi, pacu, cemmaṟiyāṭu, veḷḷāṭu pōṉṟavi piṉṉarp paḻakkap paṭṭirukkum. mānta iṉattiṉ nākarika vaḷarcciyiṉ iraṇṭāvatu niliyākiya āyar nili ivvāṟu eytap paṭṭatu. āṭumāṭukaḷ virintu perukum, atuvum mulli nilattil. ataṉāl taṉi napar cotturimi ēṟpaṭṭatu. iṉak kuḻukkaḷ, taṉittaṉik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ uruvāyiṉa.
